The first stage, Heterogeneous Graph-based Anomaly Semantic Learning (HG-ASL), extracts region-specific anomaly semantics from sparse residual structures by jointly modeling spatial and attribute dependencies across urban regions. The second stage, Anomaly Semantic-guided Diffusion (AS-Diff), injects the learned anomaly semantics into the denoising process to generate realistic consumption sequences while preserving anomalous patterns. This design enables controllable generation for individual regions and scales naturally to city-wide settings. We evaluate SynEnergy on four real-world energy consumption datasets against 11 general-purpose and energy-specific generation baselines. Experimental results show that SynEnergy improves anomaly preservation fidelity by an average of 12.21% and downstream quality by 2.96%, while maintaining competitive overall generation fidelity compared to baselines.

</details>


### [211] [SMOPD: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03092)
*Wen Wang,Jiahua Bao,Tu Yongsiqi,Yihao Liu,Haotian Zhou,Haoxuan Ma,Mengyu Zhou,Wenkui Fan,Junwei He,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SMOPD方法，通过两阶段训练解决多奖励强化学习中稀疏奖励优化信号不足的问题，在互补和冲突奖励设置上均优于GDPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GDPO方法虽然通过分别归一化每个奖励维度缓解了奖励信号相互掩盖的问题，但在处理不同粒度奖励（如细粒度稠密奖励0.1-1.0和二元稀疏奖励0/1）时仍难以平衡，稀疏奖励提供的优化信号不足，导致其对应能力无法有效强化。

Method: SMOPD采用两阶段训练：Stage1-Specialize阶段利用奖励优先级配置训练多个奖励专用教师模型，使每个奖励在其信号能有效驱动优化的条件下被学习；Stage2-Merge阶段通过在线策略蒸馏将这些教师的奖励专用能力融合到单一学生策略中，同时保持任务级优化的平衡。

Result: 在工具调用准确率与格式（互补奖励）以及有益性与无害性（冲突奖励）两个多奖励设置上，SMOPD在1.5B、3B和7B三种骨干模型规模上均超越了GDPO基线。

Conclusion: SMOPD通过专用化训练和在线蒸馏融合，有效解决了多奖励RL中不同粒度奖励信号平衡难题，实现了稀疏奖励能力的强化而不损害稠密奖励已学能力，是一种通用且有效的多奖励优化方法。

Abstract: We aim to improve model performance in multi-reward reinforcement learning training process. Existing Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO) has mitigated the issue of reward signals masking one another during direct scalarization by normalizing each reward dimension separately before aggregation. However, our experiments show that GDPO still struggles to balance reward signals with different granularities. Specifically, in some particular training tasks, the model may receive a dense reward that assigns fine-grained scores ranging from 0.1 to 1.0, together with a sparse reward that provides only binary feedback of either 0 or 1. In such cases, we find that the sparse reward may provide an insufficient optimization signal, preventing its corresponding capability from being effectively reinforced. Therefore, how can we strengthen the optimization signal from the sparse reward without sacrificing the capability already learned from the fine-grained reward? To overcome this limitation, we propose Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation (SMOPD), a two-stage training method for multi-reward optimization. Stage1-Specialize: SMOPD first employs reward-priority configurations to train multiple reward-specialized teachers, allowing each reward to be learned under conditions where its signal can effectively drive optimization. Stage2-Merge: SMOPD then utilizes online policy distillation to combine the reward-specialized capabilities of these teachers into a single student policy, while maintaining balanced task-level optimization. To validate our method, we conduct experiments on two multi-reward settings: complementary rewards(tool-calling accuracy and format) and conflicting rewards (helpful and harmless rewards). Based on above settings, SMOPD outperforms GDPO across 1.5B, 3B and 7B backbones.

</details>


### [212] [Convex-Hull-Neighborhood Smooth Dual Generalization: Controlling Local Correction Propagation in Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03108)
*Yi Yang,Zhennan Chen,Mingfeng Lv,Hanlei Li,Zhengsen Ruan,Lvqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CSDG 方法，将 Bellman 备份分解为样本内价值目标加上凸包邻域局部修正，通过平滑样本内和 OOD 导向候选实现，理论给出一步修正恒等式、迭代界和不动点界，实验在 Gym-MuJoCo 和 AntMaze 上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习可从邻近的分布外（OOD）动作受益，但这些动作的估计误差会被自举放大。现有正则化和局部泛化方法分别控制可接受的 OOD 区域或泛化目标的影响，缺乏统一机制。

Method: 提出凸包邻域平滑对偶泛化（CSDG），将 Bellman 备份表示为样本内价值目标加上 CHN 局部修正。修正通过在不同扰动半径下采样的样本内导向和 OOD 导向候选进行平滑获得。混合系数 lambda 控制修正贡献，递归折扣保持 gamma。理论推导一步修正恒等式、时变迭代界和仅依赖不动点处分支差异的不动点界，刻画隐式策略并给出条件非退化准则。实际算法采用不对称有界噪声和期望回归近似，无需精确支持分类或额外悲观 OOD 惩罚。

Result: 在 Gym-MuJoCo 和 AntMaze 基准上实现强聚合性能和稳定价值估计。

Conclusion: CSDG 通过显式分离样本内参考路径与泛化贡献，统一控制 OOD 区域与泛化影响，理论保证收敛性能，实践中无需额外悲观惩罚即可取得优异表现。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (offline RL) can benefit from nearby out-of-distribution (OOD) actions, but estimation errors at these actions may be amplified by bootstrapping. Existing regularization and local-generalization methods control either the admissible OOD region or the influence of generalized targets, often through separate mechanisms. We propose Convex Hull Neighborhood Smooth Dual Generalization (CSDG), which expresses the Bellman backup as an in-sample value target plus a CHN-local correction. This formulation makes the generalized contribution explicit and separates it from the in-sample reference path. The correction is obtained by smoothing in-sample-oriented and OOD-oriented candidates sampled at different perturbation radii. A mixture coefficient lambda scales its contribution to each backup, while the recursive discount remains gamma. Under boundedness and fixed perturbation kernels, we derive an exact one-step correction identity, a time-varying iterate bound, and a fixed-point bound that depends only on the branch discrepancy at the fixed point. We further characterize the implicit policies induced by the idealized operators and give a conditional non-degradation criterion. The practical algorithm approximates these quantities using asymmetric bounded noise and expectile regression, without exact support classification or an additional pessimistic OOD penalty. Experiments on Gym-MuJoCo and AntMaze show strong aggregate performance and stable value estimation. Code is available at: https://github.com/YOUNG-fnxm/CSDG

</details>


### [213] [Double Descent in Gradient Boosting Decision Trees via Split-Candidate Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03111)
*Ryuichi Kanoh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将分裂候选数作为梯度提升决策树（GBDT）的单轴容量参数，通过经验树核诊断揭示双重下降现象：测试误差在中等分裂候选预算处峰值后下降，实验在XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost上验证，而随机森林单调改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重下降通常通过显式容量参数（如神经网络宽度）研究，但GBDT缺乏类似的单轴容量参数；本文旨在建立分裂候选数作为GBDT的操作性容量参数，以系统研究其双重下降行为。

Method: 将分裂候选数作为容量参数，固定其他训练控制；构建经验树核诊断工具，量化候选诱导路径对训练样本的分组；分析核秩向样本量增长及极小正特征值出现的噪声敏感方向；预测更深树、更大训练集、标签噪声的影响；在XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost上进行分裂候选扫描实验，并以随机森林为对照。

Result: 实验支持预测：三种GBDT均在中等分裂候选预算出现测试误差峰值（双重下降），随机森林单调改进；核秩接近样本量且出现极小正特征值时，噪声敏感方向导致峰值；更深树用更少候选达到峰值，更大训练集需要更细网格，标签噪声使峰值更显著。

Conclusion: 分裂候选缩放是研究GBDT的有效单轴容量干预；观察到的双重下降源于候选诱导几何与提升动态的交互。

Abstract: Double descent is commonly studied by scaling an explicit capacity parameter, such as neural-network width. For gradient boosting decision trees (GBDTs), however, an analogous single-axis capacity parameter has not been established. We propose the number of split candidates as an operational capacity parameter for GBDTs. Holding other training controls fixed, increasing the split-candidate budget refines the feature-quantization grid and expands the dictionary of root-to-leaf paths from which boosting selects its updates. To analyze this expansion, we construct an empirical tree-kernel diagnostic that summarizes how candidate-induced paths group the training examples. A regime in which the empirical kernel rank grows toward the sample size and very small positive eigenvalues emerge exposes noise-sensitive directions; in this regime, test error peaks before decreasing again at larger split-candidate budgets. This perspective predicts that deeper trees should reach the regime with fewer split candidates, larger training sets should require finer grids, and label noise should make the peak more pronounced. Experiments support these predictions and show test-error peaks at intermediate split-candidate budgets across XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost, whereas a random-forest control improves monotonically under the same split-candidate sweep. Taken together, our analysis and experiments support split-candidate scaling as a single-axis capacity intervention for studying GBDTs and suggest that the observed double descent arises from an interaction between candidate-induced geometry and boosting dynamics.

</details>


### [214] [Simulation-free and finite-time diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03117)
*Kentaro Kaba,Masayuki Ohzeki,Yuki Sughiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种设计扩散模型参考过程的框架，该框架能同时实现无模拟训练与有限时间生成。核心思想是先规定可解析的时变条件分布，再构造以其为边际分布的参考过程。此框架揭示了评分匹配并非扩散模型训练的基础，而是通过参考过程的逆向自然涌现；且条件流匹配是该框架在小噪声极限下的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型方法在无模拟训练（如评分匹配）与有限时间生成之间存在权衡，缺乏统一的参考过程设计原则。

Method: 提出框架：预先指定可解析的时变条件分布，构造以这些分布为边际的参考扩散过程。通过逆转该参考过程得到生成过程，训练目标自然由逆向过程推导而非预设评分匹配。

Result: 该框架同时实现了无模拟训练与有限时间生成；证明评分匹配是参考过程逆向的自然结果；证明条件流匹配是该框架在小噪声极限下的形式。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的核心不在于评分匹配，而在于参考过程的合理设计；所提框架统一了扩散模型与流匹配，为生成建模提供了更一般的理论基础。

Abstract: The performance of generative diffusion models is determined by the choice of the reference diffusion process connecting the empirical and prior distributions. Conventional approaches typically trade off simulation-free training against finite-time generation. We propose a framework for designing the reference process that achieves both simultaneously. The key idea is to prescribe tractable time-dependent conditional distributions and then construct the reference process realizing them as its marginals. This framework reveals that score matching is not fundamental to diffusion-model training but instead emerges naturally through reversal of the reference process. We further show that conditional flow matching arises as the small-noise limit of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [215] [Trajectory-Guided Forget-Recover Network for Continual LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03123)
*Zezheng Wu,Xinghe Cheng,Qinggang Zhang,Haoran Luo,Jiapu Wang,Qing Yang,Jingwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Trajectory-guided Forget-Recover Network (TFR-Net) 解决持续机器遗忘请求带来的两大挑战：遗忘知识的再现和模型容量的逐步下降。TFR-Net 通过跟踪通道级风险，分离持久目标相关通道与瞬时热点，仅抑制持久通道；同时重新激活对保留效用贡献大且遗忘风险低的休眠通道，在保留效用下降容忍度内恢复模型容量。实验表明 TFR-Net 在四个数据集上实现了更优的遗忘效果与保留效用的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中遗忘请求持续到来，现有方法面临两大问题：1) 遗忘干预可能导致目标相关计算重新分布到剩余路径，使已遗忘知识再现；2) 重复遗忘逐步降低保留效用所需的模型容量。需要一种能同时应对这两个挑战的方法。

Method: TFR-Net 采用轨迹引导的遗忘-恢复机制：跟踪跨请求的通道级风险，区分持久目标相关通道和瞬时热点通道，仅抑制持久通道；识别对保留效用贡献大且当前及历史遗忘风险低的休眠通道，在保留效用下降不超过预设容忍度时重新激活这些通道以恢复模型容量。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验显示，TFR-Net 在遗忘有效性和保留效用之间取得了比代表性基线更优的权衡。

Conclusion: TFR-Net 通过通道级风险跟踪和选择性遗忘与恢复，有效解决了持续遗忘场景下的知识再现和容量退化问题，为机器遗忘提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to eliminate the influence of sensitive data on a model. In the real world, unlearning requests arrive continually, which gives rise to two challenges. First, an unlearning intervention may redistribute target-related computation across remaining pathways, allowing previously forgotten knowledge to re-emerge. Second, repeated unlearning interventions may progressively reduce the model capacity needed to preserve retained utility. To address these challenges, we propose the Trajectory-guided Forget-Recover Network (TFR-Net). TFR-Net tracks channel-level risk across requests. It separates persistent target-related channels from transient hotspots and suppresses only the persistent ones. TFR-Net also recovers model capacity by reactivating dormant channels. These channels make strong contributions to retained utility and show low current and historical forget risk. The recovery is accepted only when retained-utility degradation remains within a predefined tolerance. Experiments on four datasets show that TFR-Net consistently achieves a more favorable trade-off between unlearning effectiveness and retained utility than representative baselines.

</details>


### [216] [Lightweight Chunk Selection for Mobile Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03148)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Wen Yu,Jiefu Chen,Xin Fu,Renjie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对移动端RAG部署中检索上下文带来的计算和内存开销问题，提出了一种轻量级的证据对齐chunk选择方法，结合LLM侧查询隐状态、MoE路由专家信号和检索chunk嵌入，通过紧凑MLP映射到证据原型空间，利用余弦相似度选择最匹配chunk，并在严格预算下引入任务感知特征选择降维。实验表明该方法在移动端适用基线上平均提升2.5%的rank-1证据选择准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过引入外部知识提升LLM事实性，但在移动和边缘设备部署时，检索上下文增加计算和内存开销。直接仅保留top-1检索chunk不可靠，因为检索相似度不等于证据充分性。现有上下文缩减方法常需额外LLM或压缩器，移动端预算下成本过高。

Method: 将轻量级RAG chunk选择建模为证据对齐问题。选择器融合三种互补特征：问题隐状态（LLM侧查询意图）、MoE路由衍生的专家信号（生成器内部路由结构）、检索chunk嵌入（候选侧证据几何）。紧凑多层感知机将这些特征映射到chunk嵌入空间中的证据原型，通过余弦相似度选择与原型最对齐的候选。为更严格部署预算，引入可选的任务感知特征选择策略降低选择器输入维度。构建基于证据充分性而非答案字符串包含的语义chunk正确性标签用于监督评估。

Result: 实验显示，所提选择器在移动端适用基线上持续提升rank-1证据选择性能，平均提高2.5%。

Conclusion: 利用LLM侧查询表示和MoE路由信息，并将其与检索侧候选嵌入对齐，是一种有效且参数高效的移动端适用RAG chunk选择策略。

Abstract: RAG improves the factual grounding of LLM by incorporating external knowledge, but deploying RAG on mobile and edge devices remains challenging because retrieved context increases computation and memory. A direct way to reduce this cost is to retain only one retrieved chunk before generation, but the top-ranked retrieved chunk is not always the most evidence-supporting one, since retrieval similarity does not necessarily imply evidential sufficiency. Existing context-reduction methods can improve context quality, but often require additional LLMs or compressors that are costly under a strict mobile budget. In this paper, we study lightweight RAG chunk selection as an evidence-alignment problem. Our selector combines three complementary feature sources: question hidden states that represent LLM-side query intent, MoE routing-derived expert signals that capture the generator's internal routing structure, and retrieved chunk embeddings that preserve candidate-side evidence geometry. A compact multilayer perceptron maps these features to an evidence prototype in the chunk embedding space, and the candidate most aligned with this prototype is selected by cosine similarity. For stricter deployment budgets, we further introduce an optional task-aware feature selection strategy to reduce the selector input dimension. To support supervised evaluation, we construct semantic chunk-correctness labels based on evidence sufficiency rather than answer-string containment. Experiments show that the proposed selector consistently improves rank-1 evidence selection over mobile-applicable baselines by an average of 2.5%. These results suggest that using LLM-side query representations and MoE routing information and aligning them with retrieval-side candidate embedding is an effective and parameter-efficient strategy for mobile-applicable RAG chunk selection.

</details>


### [217] [On the Implicit Flatness Bias of Sharpness-Aware Minimization: A Linear Stability Analysis with Quantitative Hyperparameter Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03197)
*Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过线性稳定性分析，量化了 SAM 的隐式平坦极小值偏好，证明了最大 Hessian 特征值的上界 λ_max ≤ (bΓ/(2ρη²))^{1/3}，揭示了批量大小、学习率和扰动半径与平坦度的定量关系，并提出自适应调整 ρ 的 TLC-SAM 进一步降低顶部特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 通过寻找对局部对抗扰动鲁棒的参数来改善泛化，但其隐式偏向平坦极小值的定量机制尚不清晰，特别是扰动半径 ρ 通常被视为孤立的调节参数，尽管它定义了 SAM 测量尖锐度的邻域。

Method: 在插值极小值附近对小批量 SAM 进行线性稳定性分析，在局部线性化和梯度噪声对齐假设下，推导出线性稳定极小值必须满足的最大 Hessian 特征值上界。

Result: 证明了 λ_max ≤ (bΓ/(2ρη²))^{1/3} 的定量界，表明固定其他量时，更小的批量、更大的学习率或更大的 ρ 将线性稳定的 SAM 限制在更平坦的极小值；实验在 CIFAR-100 上用 ResNet-18 和 VGG-19 训练 900 个模型验证了该预测，且提出的 TLC-SAM 通过根据观测到的泰勒近似误差自适应调整 ρ，相比固定半径 SAM 进一步降低了顶部 Hessian 特征值。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了超参数的定量界和稳定性-局部性视角，为分析和设计 SAM 变体奠定了理论基础，同时揭示了 ρ 需足够大以促进平坦度，又需保持局部性以维持近似和训练稳定的必要权衡。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) improves generalization by seeking parameters whose loss is robust to local adversarial perturbations, but the quantitative mechanism underlying its implicit bias toward flat minima remains unclear. In particular, the perturbation radius $ρ$ is typically treated as an isolated tuning parameter, despite defining the neighborhood in which SAM measures sharpness. We analyze mini-batch SAM near an interpolating minimum through linear stability. Under local linearization and gradient-noise alignment assumptions, we prove that every linearly stable minimum satisfies $λ_{\max}\leq\sqrt[3]{bΓ/(2ρη^2)}$, where $λ_{\max}$ is the largest Hessian eigenvalue, $b$ is the batch size, $η$ is the learning rate, and $Γ$ bounds the gradient norm. The bound quantitatively characterizes SAM's implicit flatness bias: holding the other quantities fixed, a smaller batch size, a larger learning rate, or a larger radius restricts linearly stable SAM to flatter minima. It also exposes a necessary trade-off: $ρ$ should be large enough to promote flatness, yet remain local enough to preserve the approximation and stable training. We validate this prediction in a controlled study of 900 models on CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18 and VGG-19, where increasing $ρ$ is consistently associated with a smaller largest Hessian eigenvalue across batch-size and learning-rate settings. Finally, we instantiate the analysis in Taylor-Locality Controlled SAM (TLC-SAM), which adjusts $ρ$ using the observed Taylor-approximation error and further reduces the top Hessian eigenvalue relative to fixed-radius SAM. Our results provide quantitative hyperparameter bounds and a stability--locality perspective for analyzing and designing SAM variants.

</details>


### [218] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Self-Distilled Reward Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03223)
*Ranxu Zhang,Guinan Chen,Chenshaodong,Jinghao Lin,Xiaozhou Xu,Sunzhe,Yanyong Zhang,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ADRS（Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Self-Distilled Reward Shaping）框架，解决多轮语言智能体中稀疏轨迹级奖励难以分配中间决策信用的问题。通过逐步归一化特权 token 分数、基于置信度-回报关联的 Teacher Value Advantage (TVA) 门控，并将门控信号融入原生 RL 优势构建，实现了返回相关的 token 级信用分配，且保持推理时无需技能。实验表明 ADRS 在长时程任务上持续提升性能，且泛化至不同 RL 骨干、少数据、未见任务及长训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic RL 依赖稀疏的轨迹级奖励，难以识别哪些中间决策应获得信用。现有利用训练时特权技能提供密集监督的方法，缺乏跨交互步骤的联合校准、教师置信度与实际回报的关联、以及将信号整合进原生奖励-优势构建。

Method: ADRS 包含三大组件：1) 在每一步内对特权 token 分数进行中心化和归一化；2) 基于组内置信度-回报关联计算 Teacher Value Advantage (TVA) 门控，调制 token 分数；3) 将门控后的 token 信号融入原生 RL 的奖励-优势构建流程。整个过程保持 rollout 和推理时无需技能。

Result: 在三个交互式基准上，ADRS 在长时程任务上持续提升性能。增益在不同 RL 骨干、减少数据设置、未见任务以及延长训练中均保持。

Conclusion: ADRS 通过自蒸馏奖励塑造，实现了返回相关的 token 级信用分配，有效解决了多轮语言智能体中稀疏奖励下的信用分配难题，具有良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning enables LLM agents to learn through interaction, but sparse trajectory-level rewards reveal success without identifying which intermediate decisions deserve credit. Training-only privileged skills can provide denser supervision by allowing the same frozen policy snapshot to rescore fixed tokens from skill-free trajectories while conditioned on task-matched procedural skills. Existing methods, however, do not jointly calibrate teacher scores across interaction steps, relate teacher confidence to realized returns, and integrate the resulting signal into native reward-to-advantage construction. We introduce Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Self-Distilled Reward Shaping (ADRS), a framework for constructing return-associated token-level credit for multi-turn language agents. ADRS centers and normalizes privileged token scores within each step, modulates them with a return-associated Teacher Value Advantage (TVA) gate based on within-group confidence--return association, and incorporates the gated token signal into native RL credit construction. Together, these components determine what the teacher prefers, when that preference is return-relevant, and how it enters the native reinforcement-learning credit path, while keeping rollouts and inference skill-free. Finally, experiments across three interactive benchmarks show that ADRS consistently improves performance on long-horizon tasks, with gains persisting across RL backbones, reduced-data settings, unseen tasks, and extended training. For anonymous review, our code is available at the following the link: https://github.com/gitrxh/ADRS-arxiv

</details>


### [219] [SAKI: Score-Aware Low-Rank Key Indexing for Long-Context KV Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03228)
*Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAKI 是一种无需训练的 KV 缓存索引，通过直接保留注意力分数而非键重构质量，在多个模型上显著优于基于 PCA 的方法，提升 top-k 召回率并减少注意力分数 MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩 KV 缓存方法要么保留模型权重要么保留键方差，都不直接反映推理时使用的注意力分数。作者旨在推导秩 r 键压缩导致的预期注意力分数失真，并提出直接优化注意力分数目标的方法。

Method: 推导秩 r 键压缩的期望注意力分数失真，得到协方差加权的低秩目标；在边界条件下，控制失真可提升 top-k 召回。最优秩 r 解具有闭式非对称分解，通过协方差加权查询-键算子的 SVD 获得。提出 SAKI，一种无需训练的 KV 缓存索引，直接保留注意力分数。

Result: 在 LLaMA 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B、Mistral 7B v0.1、Llama 3.2 3B 上，SAKI 在所有测试秩下均优于键 PCA。秩 32 时，消除 PCA 剩余 top-64 召回误差的 13%-30%，LLaMA 3.1 8B 从 0.748 提升至 0.799，Qwen 2.5 7B 从 0.786 提升至 0.850。每模型 68%-89% 的注意力头得到改善，深层增益最大。预测分数 MSE 降低与实测高度相关（Pearson 0.997）。消融实验证实增益源于优化注意力分数目标而非仅协方差加权。分析揭示了仅权重、不变子空间和键重构方法可能次优的原因。

Conclusion: SAKI 通过直接优化注意力分数失真目标，实现了更有效的低秩 KV 缓存压缩，显著提升了顶 k 召回率，且无需训练，为大模型推理加速提供了新思路。

Abstract: Existing low rank KV cache methods preserve either model weights or key variance, neither of which directly reflects the attention scores used during inference. We derive the expected attention score distortion caused by rank r key compression and show that it yields a covariance weighted low rank objective. Under a margin condition, controlling this distortion also improves top k recall. The optimal rank r solution has a closed form asymmetric factorization obtained from the SVD of the covariance weighted query key operator. This motivates SAKI, a training free KV cache index that directly preserves attention scores rather than key reconstruction quality.
  Across LLaMA 3.1 8B, Qwen 2.5 7B, Mistral 7B v0.1, and Llama 3.2 3B, SAKI outperforms key PCA at every tested rank. At rank 32, it removes 13 to 30 percent of PCA's remaining top 64 recall error, including improvements from 0.748 to 0.799 on LLaMA 3.1 8B and from 0.786 to 0.850 on Qwen 2.5 7B. It improves 68 to 89 percent of attention heads per model, with the largest gains in deeper layers. Predicted score MSE reductions closely match empirical measurements, with a Pearson correlation of 0.997, while ablation studies confirm that the gains arise from optimizing the attention score objective rather than covariance weighting alone. Analysis of the scoring operator further explains why weight only, invariant subspace, and key reconstruction methods can be suboptimal.

</details>


### [220] [FinVerse: Financial Time-Series Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03259)
*Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Seunghan Lee,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 FinVerse，一个金融领域的时间序列预测基准，包含 116,897 条金融时间序列（1.711 亿观测值），其中 60,232 条（1,740 万观测值）因经济相关性被选为评估目标。FinVerse 定义 11 类指标族共 78 个评估指标，并根据每条序列的经济含义分配最合适的指标。实验评估 43 个公开时间序列基础模型发现，通用预测基准上的优异表现不一定能转化为有用的金融预测，凸显了需要面向真实决策的领域感知基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测基准多采用统一的基于误差的指标评估异构序列，但在实际决策中（如股票预测），正确预测涨跌方向往往比最小化点预测误差更直接关联收益。因此需要更贴近真实决策的领域特定评估基准。

Method: 构建 FinVerse 数据集，涵盖 116,897 条金融时间序列，基于经济相关性筛选 60,232 条作为评估目标。设计 11 个指标族共 78 个评估指标，并根据每条序列的经济意义分配最适配的指标。对 43 个公开时间序列预测基础模型进行系统评估，对比通用预测指标与 FinVerse 领域感知指标下的表现差异。

Result: 在 43 个基础模型上的实验表明，在通用预测基准（如均方误差、平均绝对误差）上表现优异的模型，在 FinVerse 的领域感知指标（如方向准确性、收益相关指标）上未必表现良好。强性能在通用标准下不等于能提供有用的金融预测。

Conclusion: 研究强调了领域感知基准的必要性，主张评估目标应更接近真实决策场景。FinVerse 为金融时间序列预测提供了更现实的评估框架，推动基础模型向实际应用价值靠拢。

Abstract: As time-series foundation models have emerged, the need for benchmarks that can evaluate their forecasting ability in meaningful ways has become increasingly important. Existing time-series forecasting benchmarks provide useful standardized comparisons, but they often evaluate heterogeneous series with uniform error-based metrics. Strong performance under such metrics does not necessarily imply that a model's forecasts will support the best real-world decisions across domains. For example, in stock forecasting, correctly predicting whether a price will rise or fall can be more directly relevant to realized returns than minimizing point-wise forecast error alone. To this end, we introduce FinVerse, a finance-domain time-series forecasting benchmark that takes a first step toward more realistic evaluation. The released FinVerse data artifact contains 116,897 financial time series with 171.1M observations, of which 60,232 series with 17.4M observations are selected as evaluated targets based on their economic relevance to financial decisions. Unlike generic forecasting benchmarks that primarily emphasize uniform point-forecast or probabilistic accuracy, FinVerse defines 11 metric families comprising 78 evaluation metrics and assigns the most appropriate evaluation metrics to each individual time series based on its underlying economic meaning. Our analysis of 43 public time-series forecasting foundation models shows that strong performance under generic forecasting criteria does not necessarily translate into useful financial forecasts. This finding highlights the need for domain-aware benchmarks that evaluate models under objectives closer to real-world decision making.

</details>


### [221] [ED-DiT: Physics-Guided Diffusion Pretraining for Transferable Molecular Representations from Electron Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03260)
*Liang Shuang,Haocheng Wang,Jiayi Song,Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ED-DiT，一种基于电子密度点云的物理引导扩散Transformer，用于自监督预训练以学习可迁移的分子表示。通过重建被破坏和部分掩码的对数密度场并引入电子数一致性约束，预训练编码器可适配多种电子结构相关任务。在EDBench六个任务上，ED-DiT持续优于从头训练的同架构模型，尤其在有限监督下表现显著：分子条件电子密度预测RMSE从2.2474降至1.3753，仅用10%标签时轨道能量预测RMSE从0.0293降至0.0138。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练在学习可迁移表示方面展现强大潜力，但在基于电子密度的分子学习中仍未充分探索。电子密度提供分子电子结构的连续三维描述，捕捉局部空间模式和全局物理量。核心问题：能否利用电子密度场进行自监督预训练，学习跨多样电子结构相关任务迁移的共享表示？

Method: 提出ED-DiT，一种物理引导的扩散Transformer，在电子密度点云上进行自监督预训练。通过重建跨扩散噪声水平的被破坏和部分掩码的对数密度场学习可复用表示。引入电子数一致性约束以保持总电子质量。预训练编码器可适配性质预测、开/闭壳层分类、分子-电子密度检索和分子条件电子密度预测等任务。

Result: 在六个EDBench任务上实验表明，ED-DiT持续优于从头训练的同架构模型，尤其在有限监督下。分子条件电子密度预测RMSE从2.2474降至1.3753，超越现有基线。仅使用10%标签时，轨道能量预测RMSE从0.0293降至0.0138。

Conclusion: 物理引导的电子密度预训练能有效学习可迁移的分子表示，在多种电子结构相关任务中展现出优越的泛化能力和数据效率。

Abstract: Pretraining has shown strong potential for learning transferable representations, yet it remains underexplored for electron-density-based molecular learning. Electron density provides a continuous three-dimensional description of molecular electronic structure, capturing both local spatial patterns and global physical quantities. This raises a key question: can electron-density fields be used for self-supervised pretraining to learn a shared representation that transfers across diverse electronic-structure-related tasks? We propose ED-DiT, a physics-guided Diffusion Transformer for self-supervised pretraining on electron-density point clouds. ED-DiT learns reusable representations by reconstructing corrupted and partially masked log-density fields across diffusion noise levels. An electron-number consistency constraint is further introduced to preserve the total electronic mass. The pretrained encoder can be adapted to property prediction, open-/closed-shell classification, molecule-electron-density retrieval, and molecule-conditioned electron-density prediction. Experiments on six EDBench tasks show that ED-DiT consistently outperforms the same architecture trained from scratch, especially under limited supervision. For molecule-conditioned electron-density prediction, it reduces RMSE from 2.2474 to 1.3753 and surpasses the available baseline. With only 10% labels, it improves orbital energy prediction RMSE from 0.0293 to 0.0138. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of physics-guided electron-density pretraining for learning transferable molecular representations.

</details>


### [222] [The Ignition Is Real, and It Lives at the Readout: Latent composition, difficulty-clocked ignition, and the interface-constituted commit in a recurrent-depth reasoner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03263)
*Simon Lam-Muir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过独立复现实验验证了潜在推理模型中的“组合点火”现象，证明其为真实计算而非伪影，点火发生在词汇读出层，隐藏状态几何表现为方向骤变后冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在判断潜在推理模型中报道的“组合点火”是真实计算、工具伪影，还是源自语言训练数据的遗传现象。

Method: 从头独立复现一个已发表的3000万参数递归深度推理器（相同配方与种子），全程记录训练过程，通过预注册的全签名门控认证保真度，同时在词汇读出层和隐藏状态两个通道测量分辨率。

Result: 点火真实存在且位于读出层：到达时间随问题深度合规上升，分辨率尖锐且稳定，两个同种子但训绹轨迹发散的实现中签名可复现。承诺时刻决策边距单步跃升5.8-8.0 logits，在96%情况下超过近阈值非事件步骤的90百分位；符号边距的零穿越仅是定义性的，证据在于条件幅度。隐藏状态方向在原始几何中骤变，满足预注册准则（在解码器LayerNorm坐标中略低于门槛，故合成解码器坐标声称未确认），随后在两个坐标系中冻结（角度步长从52.9度降至1.2度，共八次迭代），后续位移以径向为主（占平方范数0.961）且对读出层影响可忽略（径向logit效应≤5.7e-6）。早先的速度槽声称被撤回：预注册归一化控制显示其依赖坐标。中间表示从未通过绑定读出层恢复（中继0.00）。所有标准在数据产生前冻结；包括本文撤回标题在内的预测台账随附代码库发布。

Conclusion: 组合点火是读出层的真实计算现象，而非伪影或训练数据遗传；隐藏状态几何表现为方向快速对齐后冻结，随后以径向位移为主且不影响读出；研究采用严格预注册和独立复现，确保了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: We test whether the "compositional ignition" reported in latent-reasoning models is real computation, an instrument artifact, or inherited from verbal training data. We grow an independent realization of a published 30M-parameter recurrent-depth reasoner from scratch (same recipe and seed), film its development, certify fidelity through a pre-registered whole-signature gate, and measure resolution in two channels at once: the vocabulary readout and the hidden state. The ignition is real and lives at the readout: arrival time rises lawfully with problem depth, resolution is sharp and holds, and the signature reproduces across two same-seed realizations with divergent training trajectories. At commitment the decision margin jumps 5.8-8.0 logits in one iteration, exceeding the 90th percentile of near-threshold non-event steps in 96% of cases; the signed margin's zero-crossing there is definitional and carries no evidential weight, so the evidence is that conditioned magnitude. The hidden-state direction snaps in raw geometry, meeting its pre-registered criterion (in the decoder's LayerNorm coordinates it attenuates just below our bar, so the composite decoder-coordinate claim is not confirmed), and then freezes in both (descriptively so in decoder coordinates; angular steps 52.9 to 1.2 degrees over eight iterations), while subsequent displacement is predominantly radial (0.961 of squared-norm) and readout-null to a measured bound (radial logit effect <=5.7e-6). An earlier velocity-trough claim is withdrawn: pre-registered normalization controls showed it coordinate-dependent. Intermediates were never recoverable through the tied readout (relay 0.00). All criteria were frozen before their data; the predictions ledger, including this paper's own withdrawn headline, ships in the companion repository.

</details>


### [223] [Noise-Aware Shrinkage for Differentially Private Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03277)
*Lele Zheng,Weifeng Kong,Xinyi Zhang,Ke Cheng,Tao Zhang,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGE，一种噪声感知收缩方法，自适应衰减私有化估计，提升DP-ZO微调大语言模型的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合式DP-ZO方法在固定尺度重构模型更新，忽略训练过程中有用信号强度变化，导致噪声主导的更新获得过大权重，降低模型效用。

Method: SAGE从观测的二阶矩减去已知高斯噪声方差估计底层信号能量，通过时序跟踪稳定估计，将当前信噪比与预热参考比较得出有界收缩因子，作为纯后处理无需额外隐私预算和模型查询。

Result: 理论分析表明收缩使二次更新风险项比线性下降项衰减更快，保留有用下降同时限制噪声主导更新影响。实验在RoBERTa-large, OPT-1.3B, OPT-6.7B上显示SAGE在大多数设置下优于基线，保持前向仅内存效率。

Conclusion: SAGE通过自适应噪声感知收缩改进DP-ZO，在相同隐私预算下提升大模型私有微调效用，无需额外开销。

Abstract: Differentially private zeroth-order optimization (DP-ZO) enables memory-efficient private fine-tuning of large language models using only forward evaluations. Existing aggregation-based DP-ZO methods reconstruct model updates at a fixed scale, ignoring that the strength of useful signals varies throughout training. Consequently, noise-dominated updates may receive excessive weight and degrade model utility. To address this issue, we propose SAGE, a noise-aware shrinkage method that adaptively attenuates privatized estimates according to their estimated signal quality. SAGE subtracts the known Gaussian noise variance from the observed second moment to estimate the underlying signal energy, stabilizes this estimate through temporal tracking, and compares its current signal-to-noise level with a warm-up reference to derive a bounded shrinkage factor. As pure post-processing, SAGE requires neither additional privacy budget nor model queries and introduces only constant additional state. Our theoretical analysis shows that shrinkage reduces the quadratic update-risk term faster than the linear descent term, preserving useful descent while limiting the influence of noise-dominated updates. Experiments on RoBERTa-large, OPT-1.3B, and OPT-6.7B demonstrate that SAGE outperforms existing baselines in most settings under the same privacy budgets while preserving the forward-only memory efficiency of DP-ZO.

</details>


### [224] [The Tell-Tale Trace: Detecting Reasoning Failures in LLMs Using Chain-of-Thought Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03291)
*Shashwat Sourav,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究显性思维链（CoT）的动态结构特征，发现推理失败表现为分布式、任务相关的推理轨迹模式变化，而非单步错误；通过标注推理功能分析SAT/UNSAT任务上的CoT动态，设计针对性提示干预将Llama3-70B准确率从13.3%提升至85%，证明不依赖语义忠实性的CoT动态分析可有效诊断并纠正模型推理失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用显性CoT监控推理正确性的方法主要评估单个中间步骤的语义正确性或一致性，忽视推理过程在轨迹层面的动态演化；且显性CoT未必忠实反映模型内部推理，需要不假设语义忠实性的诊断方法。

Method: 在可验证的布尔可满足性（SAT/UNSAT）任务上研究多种LLM，通过标注CoT句子的推理功能（如子句检查、赋值尝试、矛盾推导等）量化推理轨迹的动态结构，对比成功与失败轨迹的统计模式，并设计针对性的证明搜索提示干预。

Result: SAT任务中，错误轨迹表现为过早验证崩溃：更早进入子句检查、重复相似操作、过早终止；UNSAT任务中，模型过早倾向于错误的SAT结论：检查候选赋值而非跨构造案例推导矛盾。针对性证明搜索提示将Llama3-70B准确率从13.3%提升至85%，纠正了84.6%的错误。

Conclusion: 模型能力失效表现为分布式、任务依赖的显性推理结构变化；不依赖显性推理文本是否忠实反映内部计算的CoT动态分析，能够系统性地区分成功与失败推理，并指导有效的干预修正。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves large language model (LLM) performance while also providing an observable interface to the model's reasoning process. Existing approaches that leverage verbalized CoTs to monitor reasoning correctness, however, largely evaluate the semantic correctness or consistency of individual intermediate steps, rather than how the reasoning process evolves across the trace. As a result, failures distributed across the reasoning trajectory, rather than those localized to a single incorrect step, remain comparatively underexplored. Furthermore, verbalized CoTs need not faithfully reflect the model's internal reasoning, motivating analyses that do not treat individual statements as literal accounts of internal computation. In this work, we therefore ask whether the dynamics of visible CoT can be leveraged to systematically distinguish successful from failed reasoning without assuming such semantic faithfulness. We study a range of LLMs on verifiable Boolean satisfiability tasks with variable complexity, enabling controlled comparisons near each model's capability frontier. Tagging CoT sentences by reasoning function reveals premature verification collapse on SAT problems: incorrect traces enter clause checking earlier, repeat similar operations, and finalize sooner. On UNSAT problems, models presumptuously move towards incorrect SAT conclusions, checking candidate assignments rather than deriving contradictions across constructed cases. Subsequently, a targeted proof-search prompt intervention raises Llama3-70B accuracy from 13.3% to 85%, correcting 84.6% of these errors. These results show that capability failures can manifest as distributed, task-dependent changes in the structure of visible reasoning, and that CoT dynamics agnostic to whether the verbalized trace reflects the model's internal computations can help diagnose and correct failures.

</details>


### [225] [Provably Learning Multi-Head Attention with Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03294)
*Sunyeop Kim,Insung Kim,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从黑箱输入输出访问中学习多头 softmax 注意力机制，提出了一种无需正交子空间假设的算法，通过合并相同权重头、有理函数插值和随机查询向量，以 $O(H d^2)$ 次查询恢复规范参数，并扩展到带前馈网络的 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅在单头或多头正交子空间假设下给出恢复算法，实际中多头注意力头可能共享子空间，且子空间基未知，因此需要一种无需这些强假设的通用学习算法。

Method: 算法将具有相同 $W_h$ 的头合并为规范头（求和 $v_h$，丢弃和为零的头），通过改变 token 副本数量采样有理函数并插值分离规范头，再通过添加选定 token 向量跨查询匹配同一头；随机选择查询向量，在精确谕言机下以概率 1 恢复参数；查询复杂度为 $4Hd^2-2H+1$（已知 H）或 $4H_0d^2-2H_0+1$（仅知上界 $H_0$）；对近似输出给出参数误差界；最后扩展到含无偏 ReLU 前馈网络的单层 Transformer，在额外条件下恢复功能等价模型。

Result: 算法在精确谕言机下以概率 1 恢复规范参数对，查询数量与头数和维度呈线性关系；对于近似输出，参数误差至多为输出误差的模型相关常数倍；成功扩展至单层 Transformer 并恢复功能等价网络。

Conclusion: 本文解决了无正交假设下多头注意力参数的黑箱学习问题，给出了查询最优、鲁棒且可扩展到 Transformer 架构的算法，为注意力机制的理论分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning multi-head softmax attention from black-box input-output access. The learner may query arbitrary real-valued token sequences and observe only the scalar output at the final token. Recent work gives an algorithm using $O(d^2)$ value queries to recover the single-head parameters $(W,v)$. For multiple heads, the same work establishes identifiability under the assumption that the heads occupy pairwise orthogonal subspaces. Applying the single-head recovery algorithm separately to the heads additionally requires bases for these subspaces to be known. We recover a canonical representation by merging heads with the same $W_h$, summing their corresponding $v_h$, and discarding a merged head when this sum is zero, without these subspace assumptions. By varying the number of copies of a token, our algorithm obtains samples of a rational function whose interpolation separates the canonical heads. Additional queries formed by adding selected token vectors then match the same head across different queries. When the oracle outputs and all subsequent computations are exact, the learner chooses its query vectors at random and recovers the canonical pairs $\{(W_h,v_h):h\in[H]\}$ up to permutation with probability one. When $H$ is known, it uses exactly $4Hd^2-2H+1$ value queries of maximum length $2H+1$. If only a known upper bound $H_0$ is available, the algorithm uses $4H_0d^2-2H_0+1$ value queries of maximum length $2H_0+1$. For approximate oracle outputs, we give conditions under which the parameter error is at most a model- and query-dependent constant multiple of the output error. Finally, we extend our result to a one-layer Transformer with multi-head attention followed by a bias-free ReLU feed-forward network. Under additional conditions, we recover a functionally equivalent Transformer without relying on a separate algorithm for learning the feed-forward network.

</details>


### [226] [Any-OPD: Heterogeneous On-Policy Distillation for Flow-Matching Models via Representation-Space Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03316)
*Siming Fu,Zheming Fu,Ruizhe He,Hualiang Wang,Jie Huang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Mingchen Zhong,Weihu Huang,Xiaoxuan He,Haojun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Any-OPD 是首个针对任意潜在流匹配生成器对的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过将教师视为黑盒采样器、在模型无关的视觉表示空间比较解码输出、匹配连续噪声水平而非步索引、以及引入锚定阶段重新编码教师样本，解决了异构模型间蒸馏的难题。实验中将 12B FLUX.1-dev 蒸馏至 2.5B SD3.5-Medium，显著提升学生模型的 PickScore 和 HPSv3，达到教师水平的五分之一模型大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略蒸馏方法假设教师和学生共享相同的 VAE 潜在空间、架构和时间步网格。但当最强教师和目标学生来自不同模型家族时，这些假设不再成立：教师潜在变量无法在外部坐标系中作为目标，针对随机重绘局部细节的教师使用逐像素损失会导致模糊或发散，时间步索引在不匹配的调度中失去意义。因此需要一种不依赖这些假设的通用蒸馏框架。

Method: Any-OPD 将教师纯粹视为黑盒采样器，仅在一个冻结的、模型无关的视觉表示空间（如 CLIP 或 DINOv2 特征）中连接两个模型，比较它们独立解码的输出，从而绕过对潜在变量、特征或架构的任何假设。通过匹配连续噪声水平而非离散步索引来恢复轨迹对应关系。引入一个简短的锚定阶段，将教师生成的样本通过学生的 VAE 重新编码，确保在线策略梯度衡量的是样本质量而非域差异。

Result: 将 12B 参数的 FLUX.1-dev 蒸馏到 2.5B 参数的 SD3.5-Medium 中，学生模型的 PickScore 从 0.846 提升至 0.884，HPSv3 从 9.12 提升至 10.97，性能接近教师模型，而模型大小仅为教师的五分之一。直接的潜在回归方法完全无法训练。

Conclusion: Any-OPD 实现了任意潜在流匹配生成器之间的首个有效在线策略蒸馏，通过黑盒教师采样、模型无关视觉表示对齐、连续噪声水平匹配和锚定阶段，成功解决了异构模型间的蒸馏难题，在大幅压缩模型规模的同时保持了生成质量，优于直接潜在回归等基线方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation, in which a teacher corrects samples that the student itself generates, presupposes that the two models speak the same language: identical VAE latents, matching architectures, and a common timestep grid. We ask what happens when none of this holds, as when the strongest teacher available and the student one wishes to deploy come from different model families, and find that the standard recipes have no answer: teacher latents cannot serve as targets in a foreign coordinate system, per-pixel losses against a teacher that stochastically re-draws local detail degenerate into blur or divergence, and timestep indices lose their meaning across mismatched schedules. We present Any-OPD, to our knowledge the first framework for on-policy distillation between arbitrary pairs of latent flow-matching generators. Any-OPD treats the teacher purely as a black-box sampler and connects the two models at exactly one point: a frozen, model-agnostic vision representation in which their independently decoded outputs are compared, sidestepping every assumption about latents, features, or architecture. Trajectory correspondence is recovered by matching continuous noise levels instead of step indices, and a brief anchoring phase, in which teacher samples are re-encoded through the student's own VAE, ensures the on-policy gradient measures sample quality rather than domain mismatch. Distilling the 12B FLUX.1-dev into the 2.5B SD3.5-Medium, Any-OPD lifts the student's PickScore from 0.846 to 0.884 and HPSv3 from 9.12 to 10.97, rivaling the teacher at a fifth of its size, where direct latent regression fails to train at all.

</details>


### [227] [AS-FedBridge: Pseudo-Spike Bridge Distillation for Heterogeneous ANN-SNN Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03324)
*Shengyang Li,Yiting Dong,Liuyang Song,Ximing Wang,Luyuan Xie,Cong Li,Qingni Shen,Zhaofei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AS-FedBridge提出了一种面向混合ANN-SNN客户端的联邦学习框架，通过轻量级Bridge和伪脉冲接口解决ANN连续激活与SNN离散脉冲的表征错位问题，在四个数据集上实现了优于现有异构FL方法的精度，同时兼顾模型性能与资源效率的可控权衡，计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习结合脉冲神经网络（SNN）可在资源受限边缘设备上实现高能效训练，但ANN的连续实值激活与SNN的离散时空脉冲存在语义鸿沟，导致联合训练时的表征错位，阻碍了混合ANN-SNN联邦学习的实际部署。

Method: 提出AS-FedBridge框架，核心是一个轻量级Bridge模块，配备伪脉冲接口（Pseudo-Spike Interface），将ANN的连续信号投影到脉冲兼容空间，实现ANN与SNN的表征对齐；并建立了首个混合ANN-SNN联邦学习综合基准用于评估。

Result: 实验证明ANN-SNN对齐程度与协作FL性能呈正相关。在四个数据集上，AS-FedBridge持续超越多种先进异构FL方法，有效缓解了极端规模、架构及客户端异质性挑战，并实现了模型性能与资源效率的高度可控权衡，仅引入边际计算开销。

Conclusion: AS-FedBridge为混合ANN-SNN联邦学习系统提供了一个鲁棒且实用的基础，解决了表征错位这一核心障碍，推动了脉冲神经网络在联邦学习边缘部署中的应用前景。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across distributed edge devices while strictly preserving data privacy. To facilitate practical deployment on resource-constrained edge devices, Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as a promising alternative to traditional Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs) due to their sparse computing mechanisms and high energy efficiency. However, jointly training ANNs and SNNs exposes a challenge of representational misalignment, which is intrinsically caused by differences in information representation, specifically the semantic gap between continuous real-valued activations in ANNs and discrete spatio-temporal spikes in SNNs. To overcome this barrier, we propose AS-FedBridge, a novel federated learning framework tailored for mixed ANN-SNN clients. AS-FedBridge features a lightweight Bridge equipped with a Pseudo-Spike Interface, which effectively projects continuous signals into a spike-compatible space to facilitate ANN-SNN alignment. Given the absence of existing mixed ANN-SNN federated frameworks, we establish a comprehensive benchmark to evaluate against multiple advanced heterogeneous FL methods. Our empirical analysis demonstrates a positive correlation between the degree of ANN-SNN alignment and the collaborative FL performance. Across four datasets, AS-FedBridge consistently demonstrates advanced accuracy while mitigating extreme scale, architecture, and client heterogeneity challenge. Furthermore, our framework enables a highly controllable trade-off between model performance and resource efficiency. AS-FedBridge accomplishes these robust performance gains while introducing only marginal computational overhead, establishing a robust and practical foundation for mixed ANN-SNN federated learning systems.

</details>


### [228] [Tight Worst-Case Bounds for the Smallest Eigenvalue of ReLU NTK Gram Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03368)
*Zhao Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵H的最小特征值，证明其与投影分离度Δ±的关系为λ_min(H) = Θ(Δ±/√log n)，即下界和上界在常数因子下匹配，确立了紧致的无维度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ReLU激活函数导数Gram矩阵的谱性质对神经网络理论（如无限宽度极限、核方法、优化景观）至关重要。现有结果多依赖维度或特定分布，本文旨在建立通用的无维度下界，并证明其最优性。

Method: 利用高斯方向上的门控内积平均定义Gram矩阵H；通过概率方法（如矩阵浓度不等式、覆盖数论证）建立下界；构造最坏情况向量族（如正交基扰动、对称配置）证明上界匹配。

Result: 证明通用下界λ_min(H) = Ω(Δ±/√log n)；构造最坏情况族使得λ_min(H) = O(Δ±/√log n)，表明该速率在通用常数下最优。

Conclusion: 连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵的最小特征值由投影分离度Δ±决定，其最优缩放为Θ(Δ±/√log n)，该界无维度依赖且常数因子紧致。

Abstract: For $n$ unit vectors $x_1,\ldots,x_n \in \mathbb{R}^d$, we study the continuous ReLU derivative Gram matrix $H$, whose entries are obtained by averaging pairwise gated inner products over a standard Gaussian direction. Writing $ Δ_\pm := \min_{i \neq j} \min\{ \|x_i-x_j\|_2, \|x_i+x_j\|_2 \} $ for their projective separation, we prove the universal dimension-free lower bound $ λ_{\min}(H) = Ω( Δ_\pm/\sqrt{\log n} ) $. Conversely, we construct worst-case families satisfying the matching upper bound $ λ_{\min}(H) = O( Δ_\pm/\sqrt{\log n} ) $, showing that this rate is tight up to universal constants.

</details>


### [229] [Benign interpolation and Occam's razor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03386)
*Tom F. Sterkenburg,Daniel A. Herrmann,Jan-Willem Romeijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，深度学习中良性插值现象无法用经典统计学习理论解释，新解释常诉诸插值模型间的“简洁性偏好”，但这造成了解释鸿沟：经典理论通过定理将模型类的简洁性与泛化性能联系起来，而新解释仅诉诸单个模型的性质并将其诠释为简洁性，缺乏可证明的泛化联系，使得一个未经论证的实质性假设伪装成熟悉的方法论原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为哲学受众澄清良性插值争论，并揭示新解释中诉诸简洁性所造成的解释鸿沟。

Method: 通过哲学分析对比经典统计学习理论与新近关于良性插值的解释，考察二者如何将简洁性与泛化性能联系起来。

Result: 经典理论提供定理保证模型类简洁性导致良好泛化，支撑方法论简洁性规范；新解释则诉诸单个插值模型的性质并将其称为简洁性，但缺乏通向泛化的可证明联系，“简洁性”一词取代了定理的作用，使实质性假设看似方法论原则。

Conclusion: 新解释中对简洁性的诉诸并无经典理论那种定理支撑，存在将未经论证的假设包装为方法论原则的风险，需进一步建立单个模型简洁性性质与泛化性能之间的严格联系。

Abstract: Contemporary deep learning methods generalize well even when they fit their training data perfectly, a phenomenon known as benign interpolation. This phenomenon cannot be accounted for by classical statistical learning theory and has prompted a range of attempted new explanations in the statistics and machine learning literature. A common feature of these new proposals is an appeal to a simplicity preference among interpolating models, often presented as a form of Occam's razor. We clarify this debate for a philosophical audience and argue that this new appeal to simplicity creates an explanatory gap. The classical theory offers theorems which connect the simplicity of model classes to good generalization, thus underwriting methodological simplicity norms. The new accounts instead appeal to properties of individual models, which they interpret as a kind of simplicity. Lacking a provable connection to generalization, it is the name "simplicity" that does the work a theorem used to do, making a substantive and unargued assumption look like the application of a familiar methodological principle.

</details>


### [230] [TimeRLM: Recursive Language Models Enable Precise Anomaly Localization in Long-Context Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03391)
*Nicolas Zumarraga,Lorenzo Steno,Ning Wang,Max Rosenblattl,Thomas Kaar,Maxwell A. Xu,Kevin O'Sullivan,Markus Kreft,Elgar Fleisch,Paul Schmiedmayer,Patrick Langer,Robert Jakob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TimeRLM，一种基于递归语言模型（RLM）的时间序列异常定位方法，通过代码和视觉能力顺序处理长上下文时间序列信号，并引入了合成基准AnomalyXL。TimeRLM在定位任务上显著优于现有TSLM和单遍基线，IoU达0.682；经强化学习后训练后，交互轮数减少三分之一，且在真实ECG、睡眠及软件可观测性数据上泛化性能优于TSLM。研究表明递归交互是长时程检索的有效范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列语言模型（TSLM）在长上下文下检索性能下降，类似文本、视觉、音频领域的失效模式。文本领域的递归语言模型（RLM）通过外部上下文和代码查询恢复了性能，但尚未针对时间序列异常定位进行探索。需解决长时程高频数据中精准异常定位难题。

Method: 提出TimeRLM：基于RLM框架，利用代码工具（如滑动窗口、统计计算、可视化）和视觉能力顺序操作时间序列信号，将长上下文外部化，通过多轮代理交互精准定位异常。构建合成基准AnomalyXL（含MCQ和Localize两种变体），包含五类程序化注入异常任务。进一步采用强化学习后训练减少交互轮数。

Result: TimeRLM在AnomalyXL-Localize四个任务上超越所有基线：定位IoU达0.682（基线最多0.329），classify-with-evidence达0.745（基线最多0.072）。RL后训练模型交互轮数减少约1/3。在未见真实ECG、睡眠、软件可观测性数据上保持或提升性能，且仅在合成数据上训练。

Conclusion: 递归交互结合代码与视觉工具能有效解决长上下文时间序列精准异常定位问题。合成数据训练的模型具备强泛化能力，为长时程时间序列检索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Precise anomaly localization over long-context time series is a crucial task in monitoring applications across clinical care, industrial operations, financial services, and logistics, where brief evidence may hide inside long spans of high-frequency data. Time-Series Language Models (TSLMs) are able to ingest time series data and verbalize findings on anomalies in natural language; however, recent benchmarks report a decrease in retrieval performance at long contexts, mirroring failure modes in text, vision, and audio. In the text domain, Recursive Language Models (RLMs) can recover much of this lost performance by keeping context external to the large language model (LLM), allowing the model to query it through code. We present TimeRLM, an RLM formulation for time-series that sequentially manipulates the signal using code and vision capabilities. We further introduce AnomalyXL, a synthetic long-context anomaly localization benchmark with programmatically injected anomalies that require precise retrieval. We implement five different task categories and two variants: AnomalyXL-MCQ and AnomalyXL-Localize. TimeRLM outperforms every evaluated TSLM and single-pass baseline on four of the five AnomalyXL-Localize tasks, reaching 0.682 IoU on localization and 0.745 on classify-with-evidence, versus at most 0.329 and 0.072 across all baselines. We post-train TimeRLM using reinforcement learning. The resulting model further improves performance and requires approximately one-third as many agent interaction turns as its untrained base model to produce a final answer. On unseen real-world ECG, sleep and software observability recordings, the post-trained TimeRLM retains or improves performance, surpassing TSLMs despite being trained exclusively on synthetic data. Our findings suggest recursive interaction with time-series is an effective approach for long-horizon retrieval.

</details>


### [231] [Shorter Reasoning, Earlier Answers? An Evaluation of Reasoning Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03401)
*Francesca Carlon,Vincent Ginis,Andres Algaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在匹配推理长度下，通过提示词或训练设置缩短大模型推理轨迹对准确率的影响，发现紧截止日期下低努力或简洁指令更有利，但允许高努力完成可恢复更高最终准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在回答前常进行冗长推理，增加成本和延迟；现有提示词或训练设置虽可缩短推理，但较短轨迹可能仅反映模型更早停止，需在匹配推理长度下系统评估对准确率的影响。

Method: 在GPQA Diamond (198题) 和MMLU-Pro (500题) 上进行配对运行测试：对Qwen3-14B使用数字/简洁提示词宣布token限制；对gpt-oss-20b和-120b使用训练好的努力设置（低/中/高）。在匹配的token预算（512、2048等）下比较不同设置的推理轨迹长度、准确率、完成情况及概率分布。

Result: Qwen提示词缩短推理轨迹12-17%，匹配token限制下准确率变化小且好坏参半；简洁/早回答指令使MMLU-Pro在512 token下准确率提升3.8个百分点（含未完成运行+2.7点），2048 token下增益不确定。gpt-oss中，完成的低/中努力推理的候选logit答案比匹配长度的高努力推理准确高14.5-26.3点；512 token优势主要源于低努力更早完成，未完成运行间差异较小且好坏参半。错误早期答案常将概率集中在选中选项，更早停止不一致提升概率质量。

Conclusion: 紧截止日期下，低努力或简洁指令更有优势；允许高努力完成可恢复更高最终准确率。评估应报告：截止日期前的正确完成率、运行停止时获得的答案、未完成运行间的差异、以及分配给正确答案的概率，分别呈现。

Abstract: Large language models often reason at length before answering, increasing cost and latency. Prompts and trained settings can shorten this reasoning, but a shorter trace may only show that the model stopped sooner. Here, we evaluate paired runs of the same question at matched reasoning horizons across 198 GPQA Diamond and 500 MMLU-Pro questions. We test a numeric/concision prompt that announces a token limit for Qwen3-14B and the trained effort settings of gpt-oss-20b and -120b. The Qwen prompt shortens reasoning traces by 12-17%, while accuracy changes at matched token limits are small and mixed. A concise/early-answer instruction raises MMLU-Pro accuracy by 3.8 percentage points at 512 tokens, including +2.7 points when both runs are unfinished. Its gain at 2,048 tokens is uncertain. For gpt-oss, candidate-logit answers from completed low- and medium-effort reasoning are 14.5-26.3 points more accurate than matched-horizon high-effort answers. Most of the 512-token advantage comes from lower effort finishing earlier, while differences among unfinished runs are smaller and mixed. Wrong early answers often concentrate probability on the chosen option, so earlier stopping does not uniformly improve probability quality. In these tests, a tight deadline can favor lower effort or a concise instruction, whereas allowing high effort to finish can recover higher final accuracy. Evaluations should report correct completion before a deadline, the answer obtained when a run is stopped, differences among unfinished runs, and probability assigned to the correct answer separately.

</details>


### [232] [Stop Replacing Noise with Noise: Two-Source Reliability Assessment for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting in Label-Noise Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03432)
*Wenxiao Fan,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRACE 框架，针对基于翻新的噪声标签学习中观测标签与伪目标的隐性耦合问题，分别评估两者的可靠性，实现标签修正和样本重加权，在合成和真实噪声基准上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有翻新方法使用单一清洁度分数控制观测标签和伪目标，导致隐性互补耦合：降低对观测标签的信任会自动增加对伪目标的信任，但伪目标可能从损坏的监督中学习并复现噪声，从而用一个不可靠信号替换另一个。

Method: 提出 TRACE（Two-Source Reliability Assessment framework for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting），分别评估观测标签（使用损失拟合、浅层关系稳定性、预测一致性）和伪目标（使用模型置信度），源特定分数控制目标修正和监督强度，不假设互补可靠性。

Result: 在合成和真实噪声基准上，TRACE 改进了代表性的翻新基线，产生更可靠的伪监督。

Conclusion: TRACE 通过解耦两源可靠性评估，有效缓解了噪声标签学习中伪目标复现噪声的问题，提升了标签修正和样本重加权的性能。

Abstract: Refurbishment-based noisy-label learning mixes an observed label with a model-derived pseudo target, typically using one sample-wise cleanliness score to control both branches. This creates a hidden coupling: reducing trust in the observed label automatically increases trust in the pseudo target. We show that this complementarity can replace one unreliable signal with another because a pseudo target learned from corrupted supervision may reproduce the noise it is meant to correct. Our representation diagnostics provide a consistent account of this mismatch: noisy supervision redirects deeper layers more strongly, whereas shallower relations remain comparatively stable and provide information beyond the loss posterior. We therefore propose TRACE, a Two-Source Reliability Assessment framework for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting. TRACE assesses the observed label using loss fit, shallow relation stability, and prediction agreement, while separately assessing the pseudo target using model confidence. Its source-specific scores control target correction and supervision strength without assuming complementary reliability. Across synthetic and real-world noisy benchmarks, TRACE improves representative refurbishment baselines and yields more reliable pseudo supervision.

</details>


### [233] [Approximate Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03447)
*Yuannuo Feng,Zegang Peng,Yuxin Xie,Yubing Ye,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的近似推测解码（ASD）验证器，通过预算约束的最长前缀选择替代传统的二元首次不匹配截断，允许在可控的遗憾预算内接受不匹配，并复用连续的目标贪婪后缀，从而在不改变草稿模型且无需微调的情况下提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贪婪验证在首个不匹配处截断草稿块，丢弃了后续可能仍符合目标模型贪婪分布的token，限制了推测解码的加速效果。论文旨在通过允许有限度的不匹配并复用后续目标贪婪后缀，进一步提升固定工作负载下的吞吐量，且不引入额外的目标模型前向传播或近似决策。

Method: ASD 引入三层预算机制：局部目标 logit 遗憾门控、块级异常上限、请求级持久遗憾预算。验证器在预算约束下选择最长可接受前缀，接受选定的不匹配，随后直接复用连续的目标贪婪后缀，无需额外近似决策或目标模型前向传播。预算为零时退化为标准贪婪验证。

Result: 在七个 Qwen3-14B + DSpark-14B 任务上，ASD 平均带来 7.78% 的吞吐量提升（3.05%–15.26%）。在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B) 与 DSpark 的 FP4-to-FP8 兼容设置下，GSM8K 和 MATH-500 上验证器端接受率提升约 10%–16%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: ASD 通过轻量级的预算控制机制，在不修改草稿模型、无需微调、无额外目标模型计算的前提下，有效利用了标准验证中被丢弃的目标贪婪后缀，显著提升了推测解码的吞吐量和接受率，是一种实用且通用的加速技术。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by verifying a draft block with a target model in parallel. Under standard greedy verification, decoding stops at the first draft token that differs from the target argmax, discarding the remaining target-scored suffix. Although accepting such a mismatch changes the decoding trajectory, it can make a contiguous suffix reusable when its tokens remain target-greedy under the realized prefix. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Approximate Speculative Decoding (ASD)}, a training-free verifier that replaces binary first-mismatch truncation with budgeted longest-prefix selection. ASD accepts selected mismatches subject to a local target-logit regret gate, a per-block exception cap, and a persistent request-level regret budget, then reuses the contiguous target-greedy suffix without additional approximate decisions or target-model forward passes. ASD requires neither a new draft model nor fine-tuning, and exactly reduces to standard greedy verification when the budget is zero. Experiments show that ASD improves fixed-workload throughput by $3.05\%$--$15.26\%$ over matched strict verification and averages a $7.78\%$ gain across seven Qwen3-14B + DSpark-14B tasks. On DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B) with DSpark it also raises verifier-side acceptance by roughly $10\%$--$16\%$ on GSM8K and MATH-500 in an FP4-to-FP8 compatibility setting. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Kissmetothemoon/ASD

</details>


### [234] [Beyond the Gegenbauer Paradigm: q-Orthogonal Kernels for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03482)
*Álvaro Sánchez-Paniagua Ríos,Juan P. Llerena,Alberto Lastra,Nuria Torrado,Edmundo J. Huertas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于离散q-Hermite I多项式的新型正交多项式核函数，证明其满足Mercer定理，具有内在有界性避免梯度消失/爆炸，在20个基准数据集上表现优于经典核及其他正交多项式核，且数值稳定性和计算简洁性更优，为量子计算范式提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVM性能高度依赖核函数选择。经典RBF核虽流行，但正交多项式核提供数学可解释性并能融入结构先验知识。现有正交多项式核（如Hermite、Legendre）尚未利用q-变形理论，q-正交多项式通过变形参数q泛化经典多项式，有望构建更灵活、稳定的核函数。

Method: 引入离散q-Hermite I多项式构建q-Hermite核，形式化定义核函数并严格证明其满足Mercer定理（即核矩阵半正定）。利用q-多项式的固有有界性质，自然抑制梯度消失与爆炸，无需显式缩放机制。实现开源于GitHub。

Result: 在20个基准数据集上实验，q-Hermite核在分类精度上与RBF、多项式核及其他正交多项式核竞争，且表现出更优的数值稳定性、计算简洁性（无需额外归一化）和超参数鲁棒性。

Conclusion: q-正交多项式是核设计的有前景方向，兼具数学严谨性与工程实用性，为后续扩展至量子核方法提供概念与算法资源。

Abstract: The performance of Support Vector Machines (SVMs) critically depends on the kernel function choice, which enables implicit mapping of data into high-dimensional feature spaces. While classical kernels like Radial Basis Function (RBF) remain popular, orthogonal polynomial kernels offer mathematically interpretable alternatives that can incorporate structured prior knowledge. This work extends the orthogonal polynomial kernel paradigm by introducing a novel family based on discrete $q$-Hermite I polynomials, a class of $q$-orthogonal polynomials that generalize classical Hermite polynomials through a deformation parameter $q$. We formally define the q-Hermite kernel and establish its validity under Mercer's theorem. The kernel's inherent boundedness properties naturally prevent annihilation and explosion effects without requiring explicit scaling mechanisms. Extensive experiments across 20 benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed kernel achieves competitive performance compared to both classical kernels and other orthogonal polynomial kernels, while offering advantages in numerical stability and computational simplicity. Our results confirm that $q$-orthogonal polynomials constitute a promising direction for kernel design, bridging mathematical elegance with practical machine learning applications, that provides conceptual and algorithmic resources that may be further extended to emerging quantum computing paradigms. To facilitate full reproducibility, we provide the complete implementation and experimental pipeline in an open-access GitHub repository at https://github.com/Kokechacho/SVMs-QSVMs.

</details>


### [235] [FedCARE: A Multi-Objective Personalised Federated Learning Framework for Smart Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03498)
*Rojalini Tripathy,Padmalochan Bera,Shreya Ghosh,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCARE是一个多目标个性化联邦学习框架，通过两阶段训练（共享骨干网络的Pareto驱动多目标优化 + 客户端本地微调），解决医疗联邦中异构目标和特征空间部分重叠的问题，在真实医疗数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实医疗联邦学习场景存在非IID数据、异构临床目标（如死亡率预测、再入院减少、住院时长估计）以及部分重叠的特征空间。现有个性化联邦学习主要解决统计异质性，多目标联邦学习通常学习共享全局模型而缺乏客户端级适配，无法同时处理目标异构和特征空间差异。

Method: FedCARE采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段，利用Pareto驱动的多目标联邦优化从共同临床特征学习共享全局骨干网络；第二阶段，每个客户端独立使用私有特征和本地临床目标微调共享骨干网络，实现机构特定个性化，且无额外通信开销。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和Diabetes 130-US Hospitals两个真实医疗数据集上评估，FedCARE持续优于标准联邦学习、多目标联邦学习和个性化联邦学习基线，相比FedAvg实现高达12.5% AUROC提升和32.0% MAE降低。

Conclusion: FedCARE通过结合多目标优化与个性化微调，有效解决了医疗联邦学习中临床目标异构和特征空间部分重叠的挑战，为智能医疗服务提供了实用的联邦学习解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed healthcare institutions without centralising sensitive patient data. However, real-world healthcare federations are often characterised not only by non-IID data, but also by heterogeneous clinical objectives and partially overlapping feature spaces. Different hospitals may optimise distinct and potentially conflicting objectives, such as mortality risk prediction, readmission reduction, or length-of-stay estimation, while also retaining institution-specific clinical features that cannot be shared with other participants. Existing personalised FL methods mainly address statistical heterogeneity, whereas multi-objective FL approaches typically learn a shared global model without explicit client-level adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{FedCARE}, a multi-objective personalised FL framework for smart healthcare services. FedCARE follows a two-stage training strategy. First, it learns a shared global backbone from common clinical features using Pareto-driven multi-objective federated optimisation. Second, each client independently fine-tunes the shared backbone using its private features and local clinical objectives, enabling institution-specific personalisation without additional communication overhead. We implement FedCARE in a cloud-based client-server federated deployment on the Melbourne Research Cloud and evaluate it on two real-world healthcare datasets, MIMIC-III and Diabetes 130-US Hospitals. Experimental results show that FedCARE consistently outperforms standard FL, multi-objective FL, and personalised FL baselines, achieving up to 12.5% AUROC improvement and 32.0% MAE reduction over FedAvg.

</details>


### [236] [Robust General Utility for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03562)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出鲁棒通用效用强化学习框架，解决部署时效用函数偏离训练时的鲁棒性缺口，通过极小极大学习在不确定集内训练策略，并针对凹/非凹效用开发了两种具备收敛保证的随机算法，实验验证了理论一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用效用RL假设评估效用固定且正确指定，但实际部署中效用可能偏离训练时设定，导致鲁棒性缺口，现有工作未解决此问题。

Method: 提出鲁棒通用效用RL框架，采用极小极大形式在效用不确定集内优化策略；针对凹效用设计投影随机梯度下降-上升法，针对非凹效用设计随机近端额外梯度算法以缓解非凹性导致的病态行为。

Result: 建立两种算法的收敛保证：凹情形下达到平稳点，非凹情形下达到近似一阶平稳点；在LLM安全对齐和探索最大化任务上的实验验证了收敛行为与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该框架严格泛化标准通用效用RL，通过适当选择不确定集统一了奖励鲁棒RL、约束RL等现有框架，并提供了具有理论保证的实用算法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with general utility extends classic RL by optimizing an arbitrary utility functional of the policy-induced occupancy measure, thereby enabling a broader range of applications. However, previous work on general utility RL typically assumes the evaluation utility is fixed and correctly specified. In practice, the utility used at deployment can deviate from the training one, creating a robustness gap that prior work does not address. Motivated by this, we propose robust general-utility RL, a minimax learning framework that trains policies against utility misspecification within a prescribed uncertainty set. Our framework strictly generalizes standard general-utility RL while also providing a unified view of many existing RL frameworks, including reward-robust RL and constrained RL, through appropriate choices of the utility uncertainty set. We further develop provably convergent stochastic algorithms for two regimes. For concave utilities, we develop a projected stochastic gradient descent-ascent method and establish stationarity guarantees. For the more challenging nonconcave regime, we propose a stochastic prox-extragradient algorithm that mitigates ill-posed behavior induced by nonconcavity, with convergence guarantees to approximate first-order stationarity. Experiments on LLM safety alignment and exploration maximization tasks further corroborate the convergence behavior consistent with our theory.

</details>


### [237] [Pin Once, Swap Light: Subspace-Aligned Centroid-Residual Training for Efficient Ultra-LoRA Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03579)
*Xiang Li,Pengcheng Wang,Huazheng Wang,Saurabh Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SALT 是一种分层微调框架，通过训练共享的域质心和超低秩任务残差适配器，解决了多租户 LoRA 服务中服务效率与任务性能的矛盾，实现了高秩精度恢复、显存大幅降低和吞吐率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多租户 LoRA 服务系统面临服务效率与任务性能的困境：高秩适配器性能好但显存占用大、PCIe 传输开销大，限制扩展性；超低秩适配器 (r ≤ 2) 虽高效但性能下降。需要一种方法在保持超低秩效率的同时恢复高秩性能。

Method: SALT 分三阶段：1) 提供方在公共数据上联合训练高容量域质心，引入对齐正则器将域内任务子空间统一到统一基底；2) 用户在冻结质心之上用私有数据微调超低秩任务残差适配器；3) 推理时提供方将质心固定在 GPU 显存，按需动态换入用户的任务残差。

Result: 在不同规模 LLM 上，SALT 使用 r ≤ 2 残差恢复高秩精度，比最先进压缩基线绝对准确率提升高达 18.5%，每适配器显存减少高达 16 倍。集成到 vLLM 中，在 PCIe 带宽压力下吞吐率提升高达 51%，在 GPU 显存约束下提升 28%（Llama-3.2-3B）。

Conclusion: SALT 通过分层微调质心和残差适配器的设计，有效协调了服务效率与任务性能，为大规模多租户 LoRA 服务提供了实用的解决方案，显著降低了显存占用和 PCIe 传输开销，同时保持了高任务性能。

Abstract: Modern multi-tenant Low-Rank Adapters (LoRAs) serving systems concurrently host tens to hundreds of LoRA adapters. Though powerful, this introduces a critical system dilemma between serving efficiency and task performance: higher-rank adapters generally achieve better downstream task performance, but their GPU VRAM footprint and Host-to-Device PCIe swapping overhead severely constrain scalability. Conversely, ultra-low-rank adapters ($r \le 2$) minimize both VRAM footprint and PCIe transfer overhead, but suffer from downstream task performance degradation. To solve this problem, we propose Subspace-Aligned LoRA Training (SALT), a serving efficiency-aware hierarchical fine-tuning framework. Our solution operates in three phases. First, a provider jointly trains high-capacity domain centroids on public data within the domain using a novel alignment regularizer that coheres in-domain task subspaces into a unified basis. Next, users fine-tune ultra-low-rank task residual adapters on private data atop those frozen centroids. Finally, during inference, the provider pins the centroid in GPU VRAM and dynamically swaps in each user's task residual on demand. Across LLMs of varying scales, SALT recovers high-rank accuracy using $r \le 2$ residuals, achieving up to 18.5% absolute accuracy gains over state-of-the-art compression baselines and reducing per-adapter memory by up to 16x. When integrated into vLLM, SALT improves serving throughput by up to 51% under PCIe bandwidth pressure and 28% under GPU VRAM constraints for Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [238] [Design-Time Optimization of Deep Neural Networks for Intermittent Learning on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03589)
*Jakob Schubert,Maximilian Kasper,Maximilian Linke,Benedict Herzog,Mark Deutel,Axel Plinge,Dominik Seuss,Christopher Mutschler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向微控制器单元（MCU）上间歇性、能量自主的设备端学习的深度神经网络设计方法，结合硬件感知的能量预测模型与多目标优化，实现离线DNN架构优化，无需反复部署和在线测试。在Cortex-M4上验证自编码器异常检测任务，预测器加权绝对百分比误差为16.6%，足以支持间歇性约束下的可靠架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动应用（如太阳能供电）中，能量可能耗尽导致学习随时中断，现有方法难以在设计阶段预测能量消耗并优化DNN架构以适应间歇性计算环境，需要一种无需反复部署即可进行离线优化的方法。

Method: 提出硬件感知的能量预测模型与多目标优化（MOO）相结合的方法。能量预测器基于DNN模型的实现特定计算和内存特征，估算推理和训练的逐层能量消耗，包括间歇性检查点开销；通过MOO在设计阶段离线优化DNN架构，无需在目标MCU上反复部署和在线测试。

Result: 在Cortex-M4 MCU上使用自编码器进行异常检测验证，能量预测器达到16.6%的加权绝对百分比误差，该精度足以在间歇性约束下进行可靠的架构选择，成功弥合了多目标优化、自动化DNN设计、能量采集系统部署和间歇性学习之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该工作为边缘端真正实现自主AI奠定了基础，通过离线优化框架解决了能量采集设备上间歇性学习的关键挑战，使DNN能够在不可预测的能量供应条件下可靠运行。

Abstract: We present a method for designing deep neural networks (DNNs) for intermittent, energy-autonomous, on-device learning on microcontroller units (MCUs). In mobile applications where the energy can run out, e.g., when solar-powered, executing artificial intelligence (AI) faces a technical issue as learning can be interrupted at any time. Our approach combines a hardware-aware energy prediction model with multi-objective optimization (MOO), enabling offline DNN optimization at the design stage without repeated deployment and online testing on the target MCU. Our proposed energy predictor estimates per-layer energy consumption for both DNN inference and training, including the intermittent checkpointing overhead, based on implementation-specific compute and memory features extracted from the DNN model. We validate our approach using autoencoders for anomaly detection on a Cortex-M4 MCU, where our predictor achieves a weighted absolute percentage error of 16.6%, which is sufficient for reliable architecture selection under intermittency constraints. As a result, this work bridges the gap between MOO, automated DNN design, deployment on energy-harvesting systems, and intermittent learning, truly enabling autonomous AI at the edge.

</details>


### [239] [A Theory of Conditional Collapse under Low-Rank Weight-Space Ablations: I. The Single-Block Theory and Synthetic Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03620)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在理想化残差流模型中理论分析激活修补与权重空间消融何时一致，证明三个精确结论，并在合成任务上用小型Transformer验证，发现二者交互强度与理想化模型预测准确性强相关（Spearman -0.83），且结果可推广到多层和真实预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活修补和权重空间消融都声称能识别因果负责的组件，但它们作用于不同对象：前者针对单次前向传播，后者针对所有前向传播背后的参数。本文旨在明确这两种方法在何种条件下会达成一致。

Method: 构建理想化模型：条件计算以加性方式通过残差流 $F(x)=F_0(x)+∑_i α_i(x)v_i$，再由线性函数读出。在此模型下证明三个精确理论结果。同时在合成条件任务上训练小型Transformer，跨39种消融配置进行实验验证，并用第二个任务和架构复现。

Result: 1. 删除一组载体使匹配输入对坍缩到同一无条件输出，当且仅当删除对该对是对称的且不留外部对比；给出近似成立时的确定性误差形式。2. 修补载体按供体-接收体对比移动读出，消融载体按其绝对电平移动读出；二者互不相互界，并构造出单载体修补翻转决策而单载体消融不翻转的输入对。3. 对包含归一化和MLP的注意力头，推导出一阶交互精确公式及二阶余项；仅消融MLP时余项恒为零，消融头时一般不为零。合成实验显示测量交互与理想化模型预测准确性强秩相关（Spearman -0.83），第二任务复现该模式并出现极性反转。

Conclusion: 单残差块的交互结果可延伸至多块，合成验证已在配套论文中针对真实预训练模型测试，理论沿两个轴向进一步深化。

Abstract: Activation patching and weight-space ablation both claim a component is causally responsible for a behavior, yet they act on different objects: one forward pass versus the parameters behind every forward pass. We ask when they agree.
  We study an idealized model where a conditional computation is carried additively through a residual stream, $F(x)=F_0(x)+\sum_iα_i(x)v_i$, read out by a linear functional, and prove three exact results. First, deleting a subset of carriers collapses a matched input pair onto the same unconditional output \emph{if and only if} the removal is symmetric on the pair and leaves no outside contrast; the error is deterministic, and we give its exact form even when the two conditions hold only approximately. Second, patching a carrier moves the readout by its donor-receiver \emph{contrast}, while ablating it moves the readout by its \emph{absolute level}; neither bounds the other, and we construct pairs where every single-carrier patch flips the decision while no single-carrier ablation does. Third, for an attention head composed with its own layer's normalization and MLP, we derive an exact first-order interaction formula with a provably second-order remainder, vanishing identically when only the MLP is ablated but not, in general, when a head is.
  Small transformers trained on a synthetic conditional task illustrate all three predictions: across thirty-nine ablation configurations the measured interaction is strongly rank-correlated with the idealized model's predictive accuracy (Spearman $-0.83$), and a second task and architecture reproduces the same pattern, including a further polarity reversal.
  The single-block interaction result extends past one residual block, and the synthetic validation is tested against a real pretrained model, in a companion paper that takes this theory further along both axes.

</details>


### [240] [ConformalShift: Targeted Event Reordering Against Adaptive ECG Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03628)
*Arash Vashagh,Yasmin Vashagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ConformalShift 攻击，通过重排序真实心跳事件（不修改波形、标签或分类器输出）利用自适应共形预测的延迟反馈机制，在 MIT-BIH 与 INCART 数据集上显著抑制了室性心跳类别的检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应共形预测虽能补救点分类器漏报的临床关键心跳类别，但其依赖延迟反馈更新阈值，导致决策对事件到达顺序敏感，存在被恶意重排序利用的风险。

Method: ConformalShift 在不改变 ECG 波形、标签、分类器得分或事件多重集的前提下，搜索真实先行事件的可行排列，使得在目标事件评估前降低室性类别的共形阈值，从而抑制其被选中。

Result: 在 MIT-BIH 确认记录上，Extra Trees 与 HistGradientBoosting 的合格目标抑制率分别达 66.7% 与 60.0%，远高于随机调度的 4.4% 与 12.0%；迁移配置在 INCART 上同样优于随机，减小位移预算会削弱攻击效果。

Conclusion: 即使波形、标签、分类器输出与事件内容均保持不变，自适应监测系统仍可能通过真实信息的时序操纵被攻陷，揭示了医疗 AI 系统在时序完整性上的新型脆弱性。

Abstract: Adaptive conformal prediction can recover clinically important heartbeat classes missed by a point classifier, but delayed feedback makes its decisions sensitive to event order. We introduce ConformalShift, a bounded event-reordering attack that suppresses the ventricular class for rescued events without modifying ECG waveforms, labels, classifier scores, or the event multiset. ConformalShift searches for feasible permutations of authentic preceding events that lower the ventricular threshold before a selected target is evaluated. On disjoint MIT--BIH confirmation records, the attack suppressed 66.7% of eligible targets for Extra Trees and 60.0% for HistGradientBoosting, compared with random-schedule rates of 4.4% and 12.0%, respectively. Transferred configurations also outperformed random scheduling on INCART, while reducing the displacement budget weakened the attack on both datasets. These results show that adaptive monitors in healthcare can be compromised through the timing of authentic information, even when waveforms, labels, classifier outputs, and event contents remain unchanged.

</details>


### [241] [POEM: Phase-Aware $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ Feature Rotation for Time Series Forecasting Under Periodicity Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03630)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Shengxuan Li,Qiming Shi,Di Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POEM利用SO(2)旋转和DPIA注意力机制解决时间序列预测中的周期性漂移问题，实现了具有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习推动了时间序列预测，但周期性漂移（周期时序和相位随时间变化）仍是挑战。现有方法主要在固定时间网格建模，难以适应相位相关的变化。

Method: 提出POEM，一种基于二维特殊正交群SO(2)的相位感知预测框架。通过学习相位校正坐标，对配对潜在特征应用可逆的SO(2)旋转以减少相位变异性。引入方向相位增量注意力（DPIA），从相似时序上下文检索历史相位增量并整合到未来相位校正中。

Result: 实验表明POEM达到具有竞争力的性能；定性可视化显示学习的相位感知变换使潜在轨迹更规则。

Conclusion: POEM通过SO(2)旋转和DPIA有效解决周期性漂移中的相位变化问题，提升时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Deep learning has advanced time series forecasting, but periodicity drift, in which cycle timing and phase vary over time, remains a challenging problem. Existing methods predominantly model these sequences on fixed time grids, suffering from a limited ability to accommodate phase-related variation. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{POEM}, a phase-aware forecasting framework based on latent feature rotation using the special orthogonal group in two dimensions, denoted by $\mathrm{SO}(2)$. POEM aims to reduce the phase-related variability by learning a phase-correction coordinate and applying an invertible $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-based rotation to paired latent features. To extrapolate this correction coordinate, Directional Phase Increment Attention (DPIA) retrieves historical phase increments from similar temporal contexts and integrates them into future phase corrections. Experiments demonstrate that POEM achieves competitive performance, while qualitative visualizations suggest that the learned phase-aware transformation makes latent trajectories more regular.

</details>


### [242] [CausalOPD: First-Wrong-Step Supervision for Distilling Causal Chain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03673)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalOPD框架，通过因果阶段课程和在线过程蒸馏，显著提升关键推理任务中的路径正确率并减少错误推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键推理任务需要步依赖的因果链，早期错误会传播且正确结论可能掩盖无效推理；大模型虽表现好但隐私、延迟、可控性需蒸馏到本地模型；标准轨迹模仿无法纠正学生自身分布上的过程错误。

Method: 知识增强教师提供基于领域因果规则、实体关系、结构约束的轨迹；学生生成on-policy轨迹，教师识别首个错误步骤（最早违反约束的转移）；从验证前缀开始，短时程强化学习修复局部失败；因果阶段课程按错误传播顺序从证据级、机制级推进到结论级。

Result: 三个领域平均路径正确率比序列级在线过程蒸馏提升23.4个百分点；正确标签错误推理率从15.7%降至4.4%；领域特定8B学生在所有领域路径正确率均超越两个专有参考模型。

Conclusion: CausalOPD通过因果感知的课程在线蒸馏，有效解决了过程错误传播和推理掩盖问题，实现了更可靠的关键推理能力迁移到小模型。

Abstract: Many critical reasoning tasks, including clinical diagnosis, legal judgment, and industrial fault diagnosis, require step-dependent causal chains in which early errors propagate and correct conclusions can mask invalid reasoning. Although large language models perform well on such tasks, privacy, latency, and controllability motivate distillation into locally deployable models. Standard trajectory imitation does not correct process errors on the student's own rollout distribution. We propose CausalOPD, a curriculum online process distillation framework. A knowledge-augmented teacher first provides trajectories grounded in domain-specific causal rules, entity relations, and structural constraints. The student then generates on-policy trajectories, and the teacher identifies the first wrong step, defined as the earliest transition that verifiably violates available constraints. Starting from the verified prefix, short-horizon reinforcement learning repairs this localized failure. A causal-stage curriculum advances from evidence-level to mechanism-level and conclusion-level errors, following their propagation order. Across three domains, CausalOPD improves average path correctness by 23.4 percentage points over sequence-level online process distillation and reduces the right-label-wrong-reasoning rate from 15.7% to 4.4%. The domain-specific 8B students also surpass both evaluated proprietary references in path correctness across all domains.

</details>


### [243] [DiagLoop: A Counterfactual Data Flywheel with Stage-Localized Reinforcement for Diagnostic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03674)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiagLoop是一个反事实数据飞轮，将编码的物理关系或临床指南转化为训练监督，通过教师-学生框架和分阶段强化学习，在无专家推理标注的情况下显著提升因果诊断模型的路径正确性，超越专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果诊断模型需解释推理路径以指导维修治疗，但严重案例稀缺、记录缺乏推理路径、数据跨配置迁移差，导致本地部署困难。

Method: 提出DiagLoop反事实数据飞轮：1)将机制族的物理关系/临床指南编码为训练监督；2)仅训练教师通过变化原因、上下文、观测生成反事实世界，独立混合检查器仅保留有效世界；3)学生进行症状抽象、因果链构建、根因归因；4)分阶段标准识别最早失败，非终端失败用有界修复探测下游能力，弱点画像指导后续数据生成；5)分阶段局部强化学习仅更新模型生成的续写，回放和保持减少遗忘；6)相同标准管辖准入、归因、奖励、再生，检查器与生成器分离。

Result: 仅用合成场景无案例级专家标注，8B模型严格路径正确性超越最强传统基线：8工业系统提升11.6分，10疾病类别提升5.5分；对照紊乱路由控制分别提升3.9和2.3分；在两领域均超越评测的专有参考模型，即使后者获得少样本示例或规范上下文。

Conclusion: DiagLoop通过将编码知识转化为反事实训练数据，结合教师-学生框架和分阶段强化学习，实现了无需专家推理标注的高性能因果诊断推理，显著优于基线和专有模型。

Abstract: Causal diagnostic models must explain how conclusions follow from evidence because diagnoses guide repairs and treatments. Yet serious cases are scarce, records rarely contain reasoning paths, and data transfer poorly across configurations, complicating local deployment. We present DiagLoop, a counterfactual data flywheel that converts codified physical relations or clinical guidelines, authored once per mechanism family, into training supervision beyond recorded cases. A training-only teacher proposes counterfactual worlds by varying causes, contexts, and observations, while an independent hybrid checker admits only valid worlds. The student reasons through symptom abstraction, causal-chain construction, and root-cause attribution. Stage-specific criteria identify its earliest failure. For nonterminal failures, a bounded repair probes downstream competence, and the resulting weakness profile guides subsequent data generation. Stage-localized reinforcement learning updates only the model-generated continuation, while replay and preservation reduce forgetting. The same criteria govern admission, attribution, reward, and regeneration through checks separate from the proposer.
