Using only synthesized scenarios and no case-level expert reasoning annotations, the resulting 8B model improves strict path correctness over the strongest conventional baseline. Gains are 11.6 points across eight industrial systems and 5.5 points across ten disease categories. Gains over a deranged-routing control are 3.9 and 2.3 points, respectively. The model also exceeds the evaluated proprietary references in both domains, even when they receive few-shot examples or the specification in context.

</details>


### [244] [LAEF: A Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation Model Towards Point-of-Care Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03690)
*Edoardo Coppola,Stefano Fiorini,Pietro Liò,Mattia Savardi,Alberto Signoroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LAEF的700万参数导联无关心电图基础模型，能够原生处理任意导联子集，无需零填充或架构修改，在点诊断场景（1-2导联）下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电图基础模型架构固定为12导联输入，而智能手表、手持心电记录仪等即时诊疗设备通常仅采集1-2导联，导致现有模型在减少导联配置下性能下降或失效。

Method: 将心电图表示为具有生理学动机的导联内和导联间连接的可变尺寸时空图，采用图注意力网络处理，自然适应激活导联数量。在920万份12导联心电图上通过随机导联采样的掩码节点建模进行预训练。

Result: 在18个下游数据集上，满导联时性能与参数量大12倍的专用12导联基线持平；在1导联场景下17/18个数据集、2导联场景下14/18个数据集优于零填充替代方案，平均AUROC提升3.2个百分点。表征分析证实优势源于架构的导联无关性，164种心血管疾病的导联重要性研究显示群体层面性能在单导联间稳定，同时保留了临床确立的导联-疾病关联。

Conclusion: LAEF作为首个导联无关心电图基础模型，解决了即时诊疗设备少导联输入的核心难题，在保持满导联高性能的同时，显著提升了少导联场景下的诊断准确性，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Point-of-care cardiac devices such as smartwatches and handheld ECG recorders typically capture 1--2 leads, yet existing ECG foundation models are architecturally constrained to fixed 12-lead inputs, degrading or failing under these reduced configurations. We introduce LAEF (Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation), a 7M-parameter ECG foundation model that can natively process any lead subset without zero-padding or architectural modification. LAEF represents ECGs as variable-size spatiotemporal graphs with physiologically motivated intra- and inter-lead connectivity, processed by a Graph Attention Network that scales naturally with active lead count.Pre-trained on 9.2M 12-lead ECGs via masked node modelling with stochastic lead sampling, LAEF learns representations robust to lead configuration. Across 18 downstream datasets, LAEF is on par with specialized 12-lead baselines over 12$\times$ larger at full lead availability. Under direct point-of-care-oriented diagnostics (1--2 leads), it outperforms all zero-padded alternatives on 17 out of 18 datasets with with a single randomly sampled lead and on 14 out of 18 with 2 leads, with an average AUROC gain of +3.2 points. Representation analysis links this advantage to architectural lead-agnosticism, and a lead-importance study across 164 cardiovascular conditions shows population-level performance is stable across single standard input leads while still recovering established clinically lead-condition associations.

</details>


### [245] [Learning and Clustering on Temporal Graphs: Principles, Primitives, and Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03696)
*Nelson Aloysio Reis de Almeida Passos,Emanuele Carlini,Salvatore Trani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时序图上的聚类问题，对比图神经网络与传统聚类算法，提出三个视角（原理、基元、池化），并利用GPU加速使谱聚类和多层模块度优化可扩展，发现算法方法适用于属性缺失或弱的场景，神经模型在结构、时间、属性信号对齐时更有效，并提出基于社区的粗粒化何时保留下游任务所需动态的核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在许多图任务上达到最先进性能，但在聚类任务上相对于成熟的描述性和推断性聚类算法的优势尚不明确，特别是在效率和恢复准确性要求下。需要从原理、基元、池化三个视角理解图学习与社区检测的联系，并解决时序图聚类的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出三个关联视角：1) 原理：通过共享谱基础和随机块模型中的可检测阈值，连接图学习与社区检测；2) 基元：利用GPU加速的时序后端，使谱聚类和多层模块度优化变得可处理；3) 池化：将有原则的社区检测视为时序图的理论奠基粗粒化算子。

Result: 实验表明，当属性缺失或较弱时，算法方法仍是合适工具——可扩展性而非准确性是主要障碍；而当结构、时间和属性信号对齐时，神经模型最具吸引力。GPU加速的基元使时序聚类可扩展，为理论奠基的池化提供路径，但也提出核心问题：基于社区的粗粒化何时能保留下游学习任务所需的动态。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一图学习与社区检测的谱理论，开发GPU加速的时序聚类基元，并将社区检测作为理论奠基的池化算子，揭示了算法方法与神经模型在不同信号条件下的适用边界，并指出了基于社区的粗粒化保留动态信息以支持下游任务的关键开放问题。

Abstract: This work focuses on the problem of learning on temporal graphs, with particular emphasis on the task of clustering: obtaining coarse-grained representations by aggregating information from nodes, edges, and temporal dynamics - a task related to pooling in machine learning on graphs, or community detection in network science. Although graph neural networks reach state-of-the-art performance across many downstream graph tasks, their advantage over established descriptive and inferential clustering algorithms is far less settled, especially under demands of efficiency and recovery accuracy. We frame this tension through three linked perspectives: principles, connecting graph learning and community detection through shared spectral foundations and detectability thresholds in stochastic block model regimes; primitives, making spectral clustering and multislice modularity optimization tractable through GPU-accelerated temporal backends; and pooling, viewing principled community detection as a theory-grounded coarse-graining operator for temporal graphs. Our results indicate that algorithmic methods remain the appropriate tool where attributes are absent or weak - scalability rather than accuracy being the binding obstacle - while neural models are most compelling when structural, temporal, and attribute signals align. By making temporal clustering scalable, GPU-accelerated primitives suggest a route toward theory-grounded pooling, while raising a central question: when does community-based coarse-graining preserve the dynamics needed for downstream learning tasks?

</details>


### [246] [To Describe or Construct Statistical Learning Models Using the Category-theoretical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03706)
*Congwei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了经典统计学习模型与算法，并引入范畴论视角以利于理解，旨在吸引数学等其他领域研究者参与统计学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习是机器学习/人工智能的主流，已产生大量可应用于实际问题的成果，并催生了许多研究课题。作者希望通过系统总结经典模型与算法，并提供范畴论视角，降低入门门槛，吸引基础数学等其他领域的研究者加入统计学习研究。

Method: 采用文献综述与理论视角结合的方式：系统梳理经典统计学习模型（如线性回归、支持向量机、神经网络等）及知名算法；引入范畴论框架，从结构化、抽象的角度统一理解这些模型。

Result: 产出一份面向初学者的统计学习模型与算法综述，并建立了基于范畴论的理解视角，为跨学科研究者提供了切入点。

Conclusion: 该报告通过经典模型总结与范畴论视角的引入，有效降低了统计学习的理论门槛，有望促进基础数学等领域研究者参与统计学习相关研究，推动跨学科融合。

Abstract: Statistical learning is a fascinating field that has long been the mainstream of machine learning/artificial intelligence. A large number of results have been produced which can be widely applied to real-world problems. It also leads to many research topics and also stimulates new research. This report summarizes some classical statistical learning models and well-known algorithms, especially for amateurs, and provides a category-theoretic perspective on understanding statistical learning models. The aim is to attract researchers from other fields, including basic mathematics, to participate in the research related to statistical learning.

</details>


### [247] [Amortized Interventional Forecasting for Multivariate CIR Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03715)
*Andreas Sauter,Sumit Sourabh,Drona Kandhai,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIR-ACTIVA框架，用于信用违约互换(CDS)点差系统的因果效应估计，解决了传统CIR模型只能捕捉相关性而非因果性的局限，通过摊销模型实现多期限冲击响应预测，并在合成数据与真实数据上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CIR模型仅能捕捉金融时间序列间的相关共动，无法识别因果影响，导致无法回答外部冲击下的系统响应问题，观测条件分布会将因果效应与历史共动混淆。

Method: 提出两大贡献：1) 用于分布因果效应估计的摊销模型，将轨迹视为带时间戳的观测，预测校准后的多期限冲击响应，无需针对每个场景重新训练；2) 因果多变量CIR数据生成过程，提供配对的观测与干预真值。在CDS点差上实例化并校准框架，在合成真值上验证有效性，并通过回测真实CDS数据评估实用性。

Result: 对比观测型和摊销因果推断基线，CIR-ACTIVA在联合分布的因果选择性和期限分辨校准度上均领先，即使干预法则随期限变化仍保持选择性，增益集中在短期限。

Conclusion: CIR-ACTIVA为耦合点差系统（如CDS压力测试）开辟了一类观测预测器无法回答的反事实查询。

Abstract: Mean-reverting dynamics are pervasive in finance, and the Cox--Ingersoll--Ross (CIR) process is a standard model for the time series they produce, from short rates to credit default swap (CDS) spreads. Yet CIR models capture only \emph{correlated} co-movement, not \emph{causal} influence between series, so they cannot answer the system's response when one series is externally shocked, which observational conditionals confound with historical co-movement. We make two contributions. First, an amortized model for distributional causal effect estimation that frames trajectories as time-stamped observations and predicts the calibrated multi-horizon shock response without retraining per scenario. Second, a causal multivariate CIR data-generating process that supplies the paired observational and interventional ground truth that real markets cannot. We instantiate and calibrate the framework on CDS spreads as a testbed. CIR-ACTIVA's validity is established on synthetic ground truth, independent of how well the simulator matches reality, while practical grounding is assessed by backtesting the generated traces against real CDS data. Against observational and amortized causal-inference baselines, CIR-ACTIVA leads on both causal selectivity in the joint distribution and horizon-resolved calibration, retaining its selectivity once the interventional law varies over the horizon, with gains concentrating at short horizons. This opens up a class of what-if queries on coupled spread systems, CDS stress testing among them, that observational forecasters cannot answer.

</details>


### [248] [UNVaMP: Neural Knowledge Tracing with Variational Regularization of Latent Knowledge Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03811)
*Carson J. Cook,Ahmed J. Zerouali,Anthony Schmidt,Reginald Ziedzor,Paul Lin,Luke G. Eglington*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一神经变分能力测量（UNVaMP）架构，融合学生-题目交互与内部记忆生成动态潜在知识表示，支持预测未来回答并控制学习轨迹平滑度。UNVaMP可配置为纯神经模型（UNVaMP-MLP）或混合可解释模型（UNVaMP-MIRT）。实验显示纯神经版本在4个数据集中3个达到最优预测性能，混合版本仅略逊一筹，表明可解释性带来的预测损失很小。此外，UNVaMP提供波动性控制、不确定性量化、异构特征支持及可解释的瞬时知识状态估计。实验与仿真验证其对真实教育系统的实用性及恢复潜在结构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识追踪方法难以同时兼顾预测准确性、学习轨迹平滑度控制、不确定性量化、异构特征融合以及可解释性。需要一种统一架构，能整合观测交互与内部记忆，生成演化的潜在知识表示，并支持显式控制估计轨迹的波动性，同时提供可解释的知识状态估计。

Method: 提出UNVaMP架构，采用变分推理框架整合学生-题目交互历史与内部记忆，输出动态潜在知识状态。提供两种配置：纯神经网络预测器（UNVaMP-MLP）和混合模型（UNVaMP-MIRT），后者在潜在空间上使用1PL多维项目反应理论（MIRT）测量函数预测回答。模型包含平滑度控制机制、不确定性量化模块，并支持灵活的异构输入特征。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，UNVaMP-MLP在三个数据集上达到最强预测性能；UNVaMP-MIRT仅略微落后，表明可解释性成本低。模型能有效控制知识状态估计的波动性，量化不确定性，利用辅助输入产生结构化预测行为变化。仿真研究显示在受控测量条件下能恢复良好的知识状态估计。

Conclusion: UNVaMP是一个统一的知识追踪框架，兼具高预测精度、轨迹平滑控制、不确定性量化、异构特征支持和可解释性。纯神经版本预测最优，混合版本以微小性能损失换取可解释的瞬时知识状态估计。实验与仿真证明其适用于真实教育系统，并能从交互数据中恢复潜在认知结构。

Abstract: We introduce the Unified Neural Variational Measurement of Proficiency (UNVaMP) architecture, a knowledge tracing method that integrates observed student-item interactions with internal memory to produce evolving latent representations of student knowledge. These representations support accurate predictions of future responses while enabling explicit control over the smoothness of estimated learning trajectories. UNVaMP can be configured as either a purely neural model or a hybrid model that predicts responses through an interpretable measurement function over the latent space. We show that a pure neural configuration (UNVaMP-MLP) achieves the strongest predictive performance among compared models on three out of four datasets. Meanwhile, a hybrid configuration (UNVaMP-MIRT, using a 1PL MIRT measurement function) lags only slightly behind UNVaMP-MLP, indicating that the predictive cost of interpretability is modest.
  Beyond predictive accuracy, UNVaMP provides the following: a principled mechanism for controlling volatility when estimating student latent variables, quantification of uncertainty over student knowledge state estimates, and flexible input specification that supports heterogeneous student-item interaction features. In addition, the hybrid UNVaMP-MIRT configuration generates interpretable moment-in-time student knowledge state estimates. Using an experimental dataset, we show that auxiliary inputs induce structured changes in the predictive behavior of UNVaMP-MIRT, consistent with sensitivity to underlying structure beyond response correctness. Furthermore, through a simulation study, we show that UNVaMP yields well-behaved knowledge state estimates under controlled measurement conditions. In total, these results indicate that UNVaMP is both useful for real-world education systems and capable of recovering underlying structure from student-item interactions.

</details>


### [249] [Resume Means Resume: A Machine-Checked Conformance Contract for Checkpoint, Interrupt, and Resume Semantics in Workflow Persistence Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03836)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RESUME CONTRACT，定义了持久化执行状态恢复的六个核心属性，通过 TLA+ 模型验证和故障矩阵分析，评估了五个主流智能体工作流框架的合规性，发现它们均违反契约，并提出参考实现 REMIT 修复了关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体工作流框架在执行状态持久化与恢复语义上定义不一致，缺乏可机器校验的契约，且实际行为甚至违背其自身文档声明，导致在中断、崩溃场景下效果执行语义不可预测。

Method: 1) 形式化定义 RESUME CONTRACT 六大属性（前缀延续、效果精确一次、分叉确定性、检查点有效性、消费一次、恢复确定性）及分叉意图、活性义务。2) 构建 TLA+ 参考语义模型并穷尽模型检查（740 万状态）。3) 设计 39 单元故障矩阵分离属性独立性。4) 开发确定性、无 LLM 的测试桩在固定版本上实测五大框架。5) 以 Verus 验证的 REMIT 参考序列器为基线修复关键单元。

Result: 五大框架无一完全满足契约：LangGraph 在中断下精确一次、崩溃下至少一次；CrewAI 违背文档重复执行已完成效果；pydantic-graph 无法从节点中崩溃恢复；无两框架共享合规画像。消费一次属性在顺序执行下成立，但并发恢复时失效（36/40 单元饱和度 1.0，跨主机复现）。REMIT 修复了分叉确定性与检查点有效性单元；跨进程消费一次单元通过读路径加锁修复，已作为可选门控随产品发布。

Conclusion: 当前主流智能体框架的恢复语义混乱且不符合最基本契约；RESUME CONTRACT 与自动化验证工具链为制定、检查恢复语义提供了基准；REMIT 证明了经验证的恢复核心可直接落地，且读路径消费声明能解决跨进程竞态。

Abstract: A framework that persists execution state so a run can be interrupted, survive a crash, and continue must decide what a resume means for effects that already fired. Five widely deployed agent workflow frameworks answer differently, none exposes a machine-checkable contract, and behavior violates even the fragments they state. The RESUME CONTRACT states six properties over the persistence API (prefix continuation, effect exactly-once, fork determinism, checkpoint validity, consume-once, recovery determinism), plus fork-intent and liveness obligations. A TLA+ model checks a reference semantics exhaustively, unchanged at scaled bounds (7.4 million states); a 39-cell fault matrix yields the separating models independence requires, and consume-once splits, its consumption clause independent of all six others. A deterministic, LLM-free harness measures them at pinned releases. LangGraph 1.2.9 durably records a second resume value and never consults it, persists schema-invalid state silently, and re-executes durably recorded work after a real SIGKILL: exactly-once across interrupts, at-least-once across crashes, on one API. CrewAI 1.15.2 re-executes completed effect-bearing methods against its written claim; pydantic-graph 1.x cannot resume after a mid-node crash; no two probed frameworks share a conformance profile. Consume-once holds sequentially and fails under concurrent delivery: k processes resuming one parked interrupt fire the gated effect k times, saturation 1.0 in 36 of 40 cells, and the failure crosses hosts. REMIT, a reference sequencer whose Verus-verified recovery core is line-identical to the shipped executable, repairs the fork and validity cells. The cross-process cell is repaired at the read path, and that repair ships: an opt-in gate claims consumption in the shared store, serving one racer and refusing the rest before any node executes.

</details>


### [250] [FedCritic-MIMO: Communication-Efficient Serverless Federated Critic Learning for Massive-MIMO Resource Control in Open and Disaggregated 6G RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03852)
*Amin Farajzadeh,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCritic-MIMO 是一种无服务器、通信高效的联邦多智能体强化学习框架，用于开放解耦 6G RAN 中的 AI 原生资源控制。各基站保留本地 Actor 与个性化 Critic，仅通过干扰感知图交换兼容的共享 Critic 参数，实现无中心化训练的协同调度、功率分配、波束成形及长期 QoS 保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复用-1 多小区大规模 MIMO OFDMA 场景下，各小区控制器需联合管理用户调度、流功率分配、波束成形、干扰抑制及长期 QoS，但受限于控制器间信令开销，传统中心化训练或参数服务器聚合不切实际。因此需要一种无中心化训练、低通信开销、能适应强干扰耦合环境的分布式协同学习机制。

Method: 提出 FedCritic-MIMO：各基站本地执行 Actor，Critic 知识经干扰感知图点对点交换。采用无线感知事件触发、自适应逐层 Top-k 稀疏 Critic 交换带误差反馈、均衡干扰感知融合。在固定策略、冻结目标的 Critic 回归模型下，建立有条件有限时间平稳性与共识收敛保证。

Result: 在强干扰耦合复用-1 仿真中，FedCritic-MIMO 在启发式、独立学习、中心化训练及通信消融基线中实现最优性能-通信权衡：最高留出吞吐量、改善用户速率分布与平均 SINR、提升 QoS 满足度、最低每比特干扰成本。Critic 通信开销较无压缩分布式交换降低 76%。

Conclusion: 服务器-less 兼容共享 Critic 参数交换能在无中心化轨迹收集或参数服务器聚合的情况下协调 RAN 控制器，验证了联邦多智能体强化学习在 6G 开放解耦 RAN 中的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes FedCritic-MIMO, a communication-efficient serverless federated multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for AI-native resource control across independently deployable cell-level controllers in open and disaggregated 6G RANs. Controllers share no trainer, retain local actors and personalized critic components, and exchange only compatible shared critic parameters. FedCritic-MIMO targets reuse-$1$ multi-cell massive-MIMO OFDMA deployments, where RAN controllers jointly manage user scheduling, per-stream power allocation, beamforming, interference, and long-term QoS with limited inter-controller signaling. Each base station locally executes its actor without centralized training or actor federation, while critic knowledge is exchanged peer-to-peer over an interference-aware graph. It enables this collaboration through wireless-aware event triggering, adaptive layer-wise top-$k$ sparse critic exchange with error feedback, and balanced interference-aware fusion. We establish conditional finite-time stationarity and consensus guarantees for the balanced, compressed peer-to-peer critic recursion under a fixed-policy, frozen-target critic-regression model. In strongly interference-coupled reuse-$1$ simulations, FedCritic-MIMO achieves the best performance-communication tradeoff among heuristic, independent-learning, centralized-training, and communication-ablation baselines. It achieves the highest held-out throughput, improves user-rate distribution and mean SINR, increases QoS satisfaction, and attains the lowest interference cost per delivered bit among learning baselines. It reduces critic-communication overhead by $76\%$ relative to uncompressed distributed critic exchange. These results demonstrate that serverless exchange of compatible shared critic parameters can coordinate RAN controllers without centralized trajectory collection or parameter-server aggregation.

</details>


### [251] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一系列球面残差归一化方法，将残差连接统一到黎曼流形上的回撤映射框架中，通过单参数族 p-SpheretNorm 实现范数保持且仅需代数运算，在 nanoGPT 上优于现有轻量级深层连接方案，且最优验证损失出现在有限 p，指数映射并非最优回撤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差连接是训练深层神经网络的关键，GeoNorm 将其置于黎曼流形上，但指数映射只是回撤映射族的一员。研究旨在探索整个回撤族在超球面上的表现，寻找仅需代数运算的范数保持方法，并统一欧几里得残差连接与 GeoNorm。

Method: 将残差流约束在超球面上，分析回撤映射族在超球面上退化为单标量设计选择，提出基于度量投影回撤的 Proj-SpheretNorm 和基于 Cayley 回撤的 Cay-SpheretNorm，两者均为 p-SpheretNorm 单参数族成员，旋转角饱和而非无界增长，p=1 和 p=2 分别对应上述两方法，恒等映射和 GeoNorm 为极限情况。

Result: 在 nanoGPT 实验中，Proj-SpheretNorm、Cay-SpheretNorm 和 GeoNorm 均优于现有轻量级深层连接方案，最佳验证损失出现在有限 p 值，表明指数映射并非球面残差流的最优回撤，而只是谱系一端。

Conclusion: 球面残差流的回撤映射设计空间可通过单参数族 p-SpheretNorm 统一，有限 p 的方法在实践中表现更优，为深层网络稳定训练提供了更高效、仅需代数运算的范数保持残差连接新范式。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [252] [Quantization Effects on Biomedical LLM Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03854)
*Anton Rasmussen,Hong Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 解码器语言模型作为分类器时，概率提取协议主导校准表现：评分规则（求和 vs 平均 log-likelihood）反转模型校准排名，BioMistral ECE 从 0.097 升至 0.289，Instruct 从 0.237 降至 0.096；提示模板选择导致 7-24 个百分点的准确率差异，可比或大于模型级效应；INT8 量化对专用模型影响 1-2%，基础模型高达 +4.2%；INT4 影响异质但非灾难性；温度缩放仅在求和评分下降低 ECE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器语言模型用作分类器时，预测类别概率依赖于实现选择（提示模板、口语化映射、评分规则），但这些很少被视为实验变量；需要受控评估这些因素对校准和准确性的影响。

Method: 在 PubMed RCT 句子分类任务（n=2000）上，对三种 Mistral-7B 变体（Base、BioMistral、Instruct）进行受控评估，使用 FP16、INT8、INT4 精度，四种答案文本提示模板；比较求和 vs 平均 token log-likelihood 评分规则；测量期望校准误差 (ECE)、准确率、F1；应用温度缩放；与微调 PubMedBERT 参考对比。

Result: 评分规则主导校准：BioMistral ECE 从 0.097 增至 0.289，Instruct 从 0.237 降至 0.096；准确率变化 <1% 对专用模型，但基础模型变化 4-6%；提示模板导致 7-24% 准确率差异，可比或大于模型级效应；某模板下 BioMistral 超过 Instruct，整体均值 Instruct 仅领先 1.3%；INT8 量化对专用模型影响 1-2%，基础模型高达 +4.2%；INT4 影响异质但非灾难性；温度缩放仅在求和评分下降低 ECE；PubMedBERT 达 82.7% 准确率但需 176k 标注样本。

Conclusion: 提示模板设计和评分归一化是评估解码器语言模型校准时的一阶实验决策；实现选择显著影响校准和准确性，需作为实验变量系统控制。

Abstract: When decoder language models are used as classifiers, predicted class probabilities depend on implementation choices, including the prompt template, verbalizer (label-to-token mapping), and scoring rule, that are rarely treated as experimental variables. We present a controlled evaluation of three Mistral-7B variants (Base, BioMistral, and Instruct) on PubMed RCT sentence classification (n=2000) under FP16, INT8, and INT4 precision using four answer-text prompt templates. Our primary finding is that the probability extraction protocol dominates apparent calibration. Switching from summed to mean token log-likelihood scoring reverses the calibration ranking between models: BioMistral average expected calibration error increases from 0.097 to 0.289, whereas Instruct decreases from 0.237 to 0.096, while accuracy changes by less than 1 percentage point for the specialized models but 4-6 percentage points for the base model. Prompt template choice produces accuracy differences of 7-24 percentage points, comparable to or larger than model-level effects. On one template, BioMistral outperforms Instruct although the overall mean favors Instruct by only 1.3 percentage points. For BioMistral and Instruct, INT8 quantization changes accuracy and F1 by only 1-2 percentage points relative to FP16, whereas the base model shows larger INT8 effects on some templates (up to +4.2 percentage points). INT4 produces heterogeneous but non-catastrophic effects. Temperature scaling reduces expected calibration error under summed scoring for both models but only for that scoring rule. A fine-tuned PubMedBERT reference achieves 82.7% accuracy but uses about 176000 labeled training examples, precluding direct comparison. These results demonstrate that prompt template design and scoring normalization are first-order experimental decisions when evaluating decoder language model calibration.

</details>


### [253] [Bi-semantic Chemical Embedder for Joint Representation Learning of SMILES and Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03855)
*David Ming Segura,Jeremy Goumaz,Joshua W. Sin,Bojana Ranković,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CheMatE，一种基于ModernBERT的化学导向嵌入模型，通过两阶段训练（持续MLM预训练+ Matryoshka对比学习）在同一表示空间联合捕捉分子结构(SMILES)与领域自然语言，解决领域自适应预训练导致的灾难性遗忘问题，在分子性质预测和科学语言理解任务上均达到竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的化学模型在领域自适应预训练时容易过拟合化学语法（SMILES），导致灾难性遗忘通用语义能力，需要一种能在同一空间统一结构与语义表示的模型。

Method: 采用ModernBERT骨干，两阶段训练：1) 在构建的SMILES标注长文本语料（FineWeb 10.4B tokens + ChemPile 11.5B tokens）上持续掩码语言建模(MLM)；2) 利用从预训练语料算法生成的SMILES-文本合成对，通过Matryoshka对比学习和多重负样本排序损失(MNRL)进行对比学习。

Result: 在分子性质预测和科学语言理解等下游任务上评估，CheMatE获得鲁棒且高迁移性的表示，性能媲美专用化学模型和通用语言模型基线。

Conclusion: 通过自定义大规模SMILES富集语料与序列训练策略的结合，CheMatE成功在单一基于文本的框架内统一结构与上下文信号，实现了双语义理解，为化学领域的表示学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Transformer models have revolutionized natural language processing (NLP), and text-based molecular representations like SMILES have successfully extended these architectures to chemistry. However, domain-adaptive pre-training often causes models to overfit to chemical syntax, catastrophically forgetting their foundational semantic capabilities. To address this challenge, we introduce CheMatE, a chemistry-oriented embedding model that jointly captures molecular structure and domain-specific natural language within the same representation space. Built on a ModernBERT backbone, CheMatE learns bi-semantic representations through a two-stage training procedure: continued masked language modeling (MLM) followed by a Matryoshka contrastive learning stage via Multiple Negative Ranking Loss (MNRL). First, we train the model using MLM on a novel, large-scale corpus of SMILES-annotated, long-context scientific documents that were constructed and curated from FineWeb and ChemPile (comprising 10.4B and 11.5B tokens, respectively). Subsequently, the model undergoes contrastive learning using a synthetic dataset of SMILES-text pairs algorithmically derived from our original training corpus. This design exposes the model to SMILES-enriched scientific literature, enabling bi-semantic understanding. We evaluate CheMatE across a range of downstream tasks covering molecular property prediction and scientific language understanding. Our results demonstrate that coupling our custom-curated datasets with this sequential training strategy yields robust, highly transferable representations. By effectively unifying structural and contextual signals within a single text-based framework, CheMatE achieves competitive performance across both specialized chemistry models and general-purpose language model baselines.

</details>


### [254] [CRS-Triage: Confidence- and Reliability-Aware Selective Triage under Incomplete Clinical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03862)
*Guan Qiang,Yushen Chen,Tianlong Liu,David Rotenberg,Ethan H. Kim,Fang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRS-Triage，一种置信度和可靠性感知的选择性分诊方法，通过分别评估结构化数据和临床文本的可靠性并结合跨模态一致性估计预测置信度，结合阈值决定是否预测或延后，且惩罚欠分诊错误以偏向过度分诊，在MIMIC-IV-ED上表现优异且对数据缺失、退化、不一致具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊分诊需要在短时间内做出可靠决策，但电子健康记录（EHR）数据（结构化数据和临床文本）常不完整、不可靠、不一致，导致现有机器学习模型难以准确预测患者分诊等级。

Method: CRS-Triage分别评估结构化数据和临床文本的可靠性，再联合考虑两模态的一致性来估计每个预测的置信度；将置信度与预设阈值比较，选择性地决定模型预测或延后处理；通过惩罚欠分诊错误，使模型倾向于分配稍高的分诊等级（过度分诊）以降低漏诊高危患者风险。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集实验中，CRS-Triage实现了强预测性能，提供了更好的风险-覆盖率权衡，并在EHR数据不完整、退化或跨模态不一致时保持可靠。

Conclusion: CRS-Triage通过置信度和可靠性感知的选择性分诊机制，有效解决了EHR数据质量问题对分诊预测的影响，显著提升了模型在真实临床场景下的鲁棒性和临床适用性。

Abstract: Emergency triage requires reliable decisions within a short time period. However, the available electronic health record (EHR) data, including structured data and clinical text, are often incomplete, unreliable, and inconsistent. This makes machine learning (ML)-based triage prediction more challenging, as existing ML models typically rely on complete and reliable EHR data to accurately predict patients' acuity levels. To address this, we propose confidence- and reliability-aware selective triage (CRS-Triage) to predict patients' acuity levels with a confidence score. By comparing the confidence score with a predefined threshold, CRS-Triage can selectively determine whether the model should make the decision or defer the case. Specifically, CRS-Triage separately evaluates the reliability of structured data and clinical text and then jointly considers the consistency between the two modalities to estimate the confidence of each prediction. Moreover, to reduce the risk of missing high-acuity patients, namely under-triage, CRS-Triage prefers to assign patients slightly higher acuity levels, namely over-triage, by penalizing under-triage errors. Experiments on the MIMIC-IV-ED dataset show that CRS-Triage achieves strong predictive performance. It also provides a better risk-coverage trade-off and remains reliable when the available EHR data are incomplete, degraded, or inconsistent across modalities.

</details>


### [255] [GENESIS: Towards Explainable Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03868)
*Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kumar Kolla,Vishak K Bhat,Harsh Vardhan Singh Chauhan,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GENESIS是一个可解释的混合因果发现框架，通过三节点结构模体评分建立透明先验，结合观测证据和领域知识，实现100%决策可追溯性，且在SHD上优于纯统计方法，性能可媲美最先进的LLM辅助方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯统计方法在小样本下难以解决结构歧义；LLM辅助混合方法虽改进结构恢复，但边决策的推理过程不透明，无法满足实际应用中每个边决策需独立证明的需求。因此形式化“决策可追溯性”需求，要求每条推断边有可审计的统计证据、马尔可夫毯一致性或显式领域推理支持。

Method: GENESIS框架分三步：1) 识别并评分三节点结构模体（链、叉、对撞器）建立透明结构先验；2) 逐步细化图，整合先验与观测证据；3) 仅在统计证据不足时调用领域知识。设计上保证每条边决策通过可审计证据源解决。

Result: 实验显示GENESIS在所有设置下实现100%决策可追溯性；在大多数基准数据集和所有样本规模下，SHD优于纯统计因果发现方法；性能与最先进的LLM辅助方法相当。

Conclusion: GENESIS将可解释性确立为因果发现的一等目标，在满足决策可追溯性的同时，保持了优于纯统计方法、媲美LLM混合方法的结构恢复性能。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) from observational data faces two fundamental challenges. First, purely statistical methods often lack the power to resolve structural ambiguities in low-sample regimes. Second, although LLM-assisted hybrid approaches improve structure recovery through semantic reasoning, the influence of that reasoning on individual edge decisions remains largely opaque. Consequently, existing hybrid methods fail to satisfy a fundamental requirement: explaining why a particular edge is included or excluded in the learned directed acyclic graph (DAG). This is critical in real-world applications, where no ground-truth DAG exists and every structural decision must be independently justified. We formalize this requirement as decision traceability, requiring every inferred edge to be supported by auditable statistical evidence, Markov Blanket consistency, or explicit domain reasoning. We propose GENESIS, an explainable hybrid CD framework that decomposes graph construction into interpretable decision points. GENESIS first identifies and scores three-node structural motifs, including chains, forks, and colliders, to establish transparent structural priors, then progressively refines the graph by integrating these priors with observational evidence, invoking domain knowledge only when statistical evidence is insufficient. By design, every edge decision is resolved through an auditable source of evidence. Experiments show that GENESIS achieves 100% decision traceability across all settings, establishing explainability as a first-class objective in causal discovery. Despite this additional requirement, GENESIS consistently outperforms purely statistical CD methods on the majority of benchmark datasets across all sample regimes in terms of Structural Hamming Distance (SHD), while achieving performance comparable to state-of-the-art LLM-assisted approaches.

</details>


### [256] [Enhancing VLM Reward Models Through Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03875)
*Pyrros Koussios,Chenhao Li,Xin Chen,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAFT的结构感知微调方法，通过自监督方式利用任务内在结构先验正则化VLM的潜在空间（使用LoRA适配器），在无需真值监督的情况下在线优化奖励信号，显著提升了RL策略的收敛速度和对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中设计有效奖励函数是主要瓶颈；现有工作利用大型视觉语言模型（VLM）作为奖励模型计算文本-观测相似度，但这些奖励往往噪声大、不可靠，限制了实际部署效用。

Method: 提出Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT)，一种简单的自监督方法，利用任务固有的结构先验通过LoRA适配器正则化VLM的潜在空间，在无需地面真值监督的情况下在线细化不完美的奖励信号。

Result: SAFT在多种基础模型能力上持续去噪奖励景观，加快策略收敛，显著提升对齐度（EPIC距离），表明失败常归因于结构脆性而非语义误解。

Conclusion: SAFT用任务固有的结构归纳偏置替代了大量人类偏好标注，为稳定文本条件RL提供了可扩展路径，并凸显了将任务结构作为通用归纳偏置的广泛价值。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions remains a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL). Recent work uses large foundation Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as reward models, computing text-observation similarity to bypass manual reward engineering. Although promising, these rewards are often noisy and unreliable, limiting their direct utility during deployment. We present Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT), a simple, self-supervised method that refines these imperfect reward signals online without access to ground-truth supervision. SAFT leverages intrinsic structural priors to regularize the VLM's latent space via LoRA adapters. We rigorously evaluate SAFT across a spectrum of base model capabilities to demonstrate its versatility. Our results show that SAFT consistently denoises the reward landscape, yielding faster policy convergence and substantially improved alignment (EPIC distance) relative to the underlying base model, suggesting that failures can often be attributed to structural brittleness rather than semantic misunderstanding. By replacing extensive human preference annotation with structural inductive biases inherent to the task, SAFT offers a scalable path for stabilizing text-conditioned RL and underscores the broader value of incorporating task structure as a general inductive bias.

</details>


### [257] [Operationally Feasible Synthetic Power-Grid Scenarios via Learning the AC-Operable Joint Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03878)
*Chenhan Xiao,Xinyu He,Haoran Li,Hanghang Tong,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于层次扩散模型的可行性感知分布学习框架，用于生成交流潮流可行的合成电力网络场景，将拓扑、支路参数和时变负荷的联合分布学习融入生成过程，无需后处理即可显著提升运行可行性和应急鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成电网生成方法虽结合工程知识通过后验证、优化或物理感知生成提升结构真实性和运行可行性，但生成场景仍常表现出低交流可行性和鲁棒性，限制了其在下游电力系统研究中的实用价值。

Method: 提出可行性感知分布学习框架，将交流潮流收敛和运行约束嵌入层次扩散模型。采用三阶段层次架构：第一阶段生成拓扑和节点属性；第二阶段在生成结构条件下生成支路电气参数；第三阶段在网络结构和电气特性条件下生成时变负荷谱。通过高效扩散采样直接生成运行可行场景。

Result: 在基准系统上实验表明，该框架显著提升运行可行性和应急鲁棒性，保持较强的统计保真度，并消除了基于优化的后处理步骤。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将工程约束融入生成式扩散学习，实现了端到端的交流可行合成电网场景生成，为电力系统规划、韧性评估和数据驱动应用提供了高质量合成数据。

Abstract: Synthetic power-grid scenarios are essential for planning, resilience assessment, contingency analysis, and data-driven power-system applications. Recent synthetic grid generation methods have improved structural realism and operational feasibility by incorporating engineering knowledge through post-generation validation, optimization, or physics-aware generation. However, generated scenarios may still exhibit low AC feasibility and robustness, limiting their practical value for downstream power-system studies. This paper proposes a feasibility-aware distribution-learning framework that learns the AC-operable joint distribution of network topology, branch electrical parameters, and time-varying load profiles. Instead of enforcing feasibility after generation, the proposed framework incorporates AC power-flow convergence and operational constraints into hierarchical diffusion-based distribution learning. This enables the generator itself to produce operationally feasible grid scenarios through efficient diffusion sampling. The hierarchical architecture decomposes the high-dimensional generation task into three engineering-motivated stages: topology and bus-attribute generation, branch-parameter generation conditioned on the generated structure, and load-profile generation conditioned on both network structure and electrical characteristics. Experiments on benchmark systems demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves operational feasibility and contingency robustness while maintaining strong statistical fidelity and eliminating optimization-based post-processing.

</details>


### [258] [Omega-S: A Functional Resilience Index for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03887)
*Alberto Acedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omega-S是一种无需旧数据、Fisher矩阵或旧权重副本的即插即用正则化方法，仅通过权重矩阵计算惩罚项，在Llama-3-8B LoRA微调中显著优于无正则化、调优权重衰减和EWC，保留率从62.9%提升至84.1%。方法本质上是对节点度方差的惩罚，且实验揭示了低秩微调中种子间0.104的保留率标准差，这为文献中所有种子配对比较提供了边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在新数据上微调时会遗忘先前学到的知识（灾难性遗忘）。现有方法如EWC需要存储旧权重或计算Fisher矩阵，且通常需要旧任务数据，增加了计算和存储开销。需要一种无需额外数据、无需存储旧权重、计算开销极低的即插即用正则化方法。

Method: Omega-S仅从当前权重矩阵计算惩罚项，基于Tr(A^3)的拓扑目标构建，但实测其四个因子中仅度方差项对权重有显著弹性（9e-3），其余三个因子弹性≤1e-4。实现上简化为对节点度方差的惩罚：方形模块中对应行幅度，非方形模块中对应方向对齐。仅需三行代码插入训练循环，步骤开销<4%。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B LoRA从代码到散文微调实验中（10个种子）：Omega-S在9/10种子上优于无正则化（pass@1 0.173→0.238，保留率62.9%→84.1%，符号检验p=0.011，Wilcoxon p=0.006）；10/10种子击败调优权重衰减（p=0.002）；8/10种子击败调优EWC（p=0.014）。同配置同种子同硬件重复实验的保留率标准差为0.104，这一数值为文献中所有种子配对比较提供了边界。对比保留对比度的构造反而使所有种子保留率变差。

Conclusion: Omega-S是一种极简、高效、无需旧数据或额外存储的正则化方法，通过惩罚权重矩阵的节点度方差有效缓解灾难性遗忘。实验严格控制变量并报告负面结果，揭示了低秩微调中固有的高方差（0.104），这对未来研究的统计显著性声称构成重要约束。代码、逐种子结果及完整负面结果记录已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a large language model on new data degrades what it previously learned. We present Omega-S, a drop-in penalty computed from the weight matrix alone: it needs no previous-task data, no Fisher matrix and no stored copy of the old weights. It is three lines in an existing training loop and adds under 4% to the cost of a step.
  Retention. On Llama-3-8B with LoRA, fine-tuned from code to prose and measured by HumanEval over ten seeds, Omega-S retains more of the original capability than no regularisation on 9 of 10 seeds (0.173 -> 0.238 absolute pass@1; sign test one-sided p=0.011, Wilcoxon p=0.006), as a retention ratio, 62.9% -> 84.1%. It also beats tuned weight decay on 10 of 10 seeds (p=0.002) and tuned EWC on 8 of 10 (p=0.014), every arm re-measured in the same session.
  Mechanism, measured rather than asserted. Omega-S is topological by construction, its objective built from Tr(A^3), but we measured which of its four factors actually moves and three do not: their elasticity with respect to the weights is at or below 1e-4, against 9e-3 for the degree-variance term. As implemented, the composite reduces to a penalty on the variance of node degrees, which means row magnitude in square modules and directional alignment in non-square ones. We report this because a method whose name promises one thing and whose gradient does another should say so. We also enumerate the open design choices, including a contrast-preserving construction that does what it was designed to do and makes retention worse on all ten seeds.
  Repeating an identical configuration, same seed and same hardware, gives a standard deviation of 0.104 in retention ratio. We have not found this quantified for low-rank fine-tuning of language models, and it bounds every seed-paired comparison in this literature, ours included.
  Code, per-seed results and the full record of negative results are available.

</details>


### [259] [Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer in LLM Families: A Closed-Form Linear Mapping for Prefill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03893)
*Taekyung Heo,Rasoul Shafipour,Ritchie Zhao,Maximilian Golub,Mohammad Mahdi Kamani,Ritika Borkar,Makesh Tarun Chandran,Pantea Zardoshti,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨模型KV缓存迁移技术，通过线性映射器复用源模型的KV缓存，实现不同规模模型间的高效切换，显著降低prefill计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产部署中常在模型家族内切换不同规模模型以实现成本-质量级联、会话中途切换和路由，但每次切换都要求接收模型从头重新计算prefill，造成巨大计算浪费。

Method: 设计闭式岭回归映射器，分三步：1) 为每个目标层选择最具预测力的top-k源层并拼接其KV作为输入；2) 从键中剥离RoPE，使映射与位置无关并可跨上下文长度复用；3) 在500条FineWeb-Edu序列（每条1024 token）的小规模校准集上拟合岭回归。针对线性映射器失效的模型对，再引入非线性MLP补偿。

Result: 在三个模型家族的六对模型上实验，线性映射器在四对上保留了接收模型独立prefill准确率的73%-98%，两对急剧下降；非线性MLP在失败案例上最高恢复+37个百分点的HellaSwag保留率。映射器运行速度比重新prefill快2.7-25倍，且在多轮对话切换中保持稳定。

Conclusion: 跨模型KV缓存迁移在实践中可行，线性映射器已能在多数模型对上达到近乎无损的迁移效果，配合非线性补偿可覆盖更广泛的模型规模组合，为生产环境的动态模型切换提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Production deployments often swap between different-sized models in a family for cost-quality cascading, mid-conversation switching, and routing, and each swap forces the receiver to repay the prefill from scratch. We propose cross-model KV cache transfer, where the receiver reuses the source's KV cache, skipping prefill. We find that cross-model KV has substantial linear structure across matched-KV pairs, where source and target share KV head count and per-head dimension. On Qwen3 14B->32B, one source layer explains 56% of variance in the target's keys and 32% in values, rising to 79% and 65% with multiple source layers. Building on this, we design a closed-form ridge mapper that operates per head and proceeds in three steps. First, for each target layer we select the top-k most predictive source layers and concatenate their KV as input. Second, we strip RoPE from the keys before mapping, so the fit is position-free and reusable across context lengths. Third, we fit ridge regression on a small calibration set of 500 FineWeb-Edu sequences of 1,024 tokens each. Surprisingly, across six pairs in three families, this linear mapper retains 73-98% of the receiver's standalone-prefill accuracy on four pairs, while two degrade sharply. A nonlinear MLP recovers up to +37 pp HellaSwag retention on the failures. The mapper runs 2.7-25x faster than re-prefill and remains stable across multi-turn handoff, making cross-model KV cache transfer practical.

</details>


### [260] [Sparse Weight Decomposition for Efficient Circuit Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03913)
*Chuanhao Yan,Xuhan Huang,Yawen Duan,Zhenfei Yin,Hang Zhao,Bryan Dai,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出稀疏权重分解(SWD)，将预训练线性投影重参数化为两个稀疏因子，共享中间坐标作为可单独寻址的电路单元，无需训练替换网络即可实现电路提取，在极少数据下达到与Transcoder等基线相当的保真度，并能用更少活跃边和单元达到相同电路充分性/必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集预训练Transformer天然缺乏可解释单元，现有方法需学习辅助稀疏表示或训练稀疏模型，带来巨大额外计算并可能引入保真度差距。

Method: 将每个权重矩阵分解为两个稀疏因子，其共享的中间坐标作为独立可寻址的电路单元；无需训练独立替换网络，支持与稀疏特征学习方法相同的评分、选择、消融工作流。

Result: 在单矩阵替换上，SWD以不到1%的训练数据匹配Transcoder等强基线的保真度；在GPT-2、Qwen2.5、Qwen3.5-27B上，相同保真度下用更少活跃读/写边和选定单元达到相同电路充分性和必要性；微调非零因子值后可全模型替换所有注意力和MLP权重矩阵；还提供零数据变体，支持更广泛的机制可解释性分析(如逐步分析)。

Conclusion: SWD通过参数化稀疏分解直接利用预训练权重，极大降低数据和计算成本，同时保持高保真度和电路提取效果，为大规模模型的机制可解释性提供了实用且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Dense pretrained transformers do not naturally expose interpretable units for circuit extraction. Existing approaches obtain such units by learning auxiliary sparse representations or training sparse models, incurring substantial additional computation while potentially introducing a fidelity gap between the representation being analyzed and the original pretrained model. We propose Sparse Weight Decomposition (SWD), which reparameterizes pretrained linear projections by factorizing each weight matrix into two sparse factors whose shared intermediate coordinates serve as individually addressable circuit units. Without training a separate replacement network, this parametric representation supports the same scoring, selection, and ablation circuit extraction workflow used for methods that learn sparse features. Across single-matrix replacements, SWD matches the held-out fidelity achieved by Transcoder and other strong baselines while using less than 1% of the data that those baselines use to train their replacements. For matched replacement fidelity, SWD reaches the same circuit sufficiency and necessity targets with fewer active read/write edges and selected units across tasks on GPT-2, Qwen2.5, and Qwen3.5-27B. We further show that SWD remains effective for full-model replacement of all attention and MLP weight matrices after fine-tuning the nonzero factor values. Finally, SWD also features a zero-data variant, allowing broader use of mechanistic interpretability analysis (e.g., per-step analysis).

</details>


### [261] [Trajectory inference via Acceleration Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03916)
*Bartolo Dazzini,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为加速度匹配(AM)的新算法，用于轨迹推断问题。该方法将插值问题提升到相空间，通过回归显式条件加速度场来生成平滑轨迹，仅需位置数据训练，避免了昂贵的预处理和轨迹模拟，在基准测试中表现优于或相当于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断是许多科学领域的基础问题，现有算法存在计算挑战：要么依赖预处理子程序强制平滑性，要么依赖基于模拟的训练目标，两者都计算昂贵。需要一种避免这些开销的新方法。

Method: 提出加速度匹配(AM)算法：将原始插值问题提升到相空间，回归显式条件加速度场，该加速度场诱导出符合给定边缘分布的随机平滑轨迹。训练仅需位置数据，无需轨迹模拟，也无昂贵预处理。

Result: 在现有文献的多个基准问题上提供了充分数值证据，表明AM算法与现有算法相比具有竞争力或更优。

Conclusion: AM算法通过将问题提升到相空间并匹配加速度场，成功克服了现有轨迹推断方法计算昂贵的局限性，实现了高效训练且性能优异。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>


### [262] [PRISM: Powerful Time Series to Image (TS2I) Representations for Multivariate Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03926)
*Mateusz Smendowski,Kamil Faber,Piotr Nawrocki,Nathalie Japkowicz,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRISM 元工作流，系统研究将多变量时间序列映射为多通道图像并使用视觉骨干网络进行异常检测。实验表明，设计良好的 PRISM 配置在 14 个数据集中 10 个上达到最佳 VUS-PR，平均比最强基线提升 41%。关键发现通道化设计至关重要，提出的统计方案 MSM 比 PCA 方案提升 11-27%。ImageNet 预训练编码器有效迁移，冻结编码器保留 92% 微调性能且训练快 1.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测（TSAD）性能高度依赖表示选择。虽然将时间序列转为图像在预测和分类中取得成功，但如何将高维多变量序列映射为多通道图像、以及视觉骨干网络能否匹配时域基线仍不清楚。

Method: 提出 PRISM，一种即插即用的元工作流，支持系统构建和评估基于图像的多变量 TSAD 表示。引入 MSM（统计多尺度映射）作为新颖的通道化方案，利用统计特征构建图像通道。采用 ImageNet 预训练的视觉编码器，并对比冻结与微调两种迁移学习策略。

Result: 超过 7000 次实验显示：PRISM 配置在 14 个数据集中 10 个上获得最佳 VUS-PR，平均比最强竞争方法提升 41%。MSM 通道化方案比基于 PCA 的替代方案提升 11-27%。ImageNet 预训练编码器冻结后保留 92% 微调性能，训练速度加快 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: PRISM 证明了基于图像的表示在多变量 TSAD 中可与时域方法竞争。通道化是此前研究不足的关键设计维度，统计驱动的 MSM 显著优于 PCA。预训练视觉编码器能有效迁移到 TSAD，冻结编码器提供极具性价比的替代方案。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) underpins applications in predictive maintenance, finance, and cloud computing, however performance remains sensitive to representation choices, especially in multivariate settings. While transforming time series into images has shown success in forecasting and classification, it remains unclear how multivariate, high-dimensional series should be mapped to multi-channel images and whether vision backbones can match time-domain baselines in TSAD. We introduce PRISM, a plug-and-play meta-workflow enabling systematic construction and evaluation of image-based representations for multivariate TSAD. Our evaluation spanning over 7,000 experiments shows that well-designed PRISM configurations are competitive with 24 time-domain baselines, achieving the best VUS-PR on 10 of 14 datasets, with an average improvement of 41% over the best competing method on those datasets. Further, we identify channelization - how the channel dimension of multi-channel images is constructed - as a critical and previously understudied design dimension, and introduce MSM, a novel statistics-based scheme achieving 11-27% gains over PCA-based alternatives. Finally, ImageNet-pretrained encoders transfer effectively to TSAD, with frozen encoders retaining 92% of fine-tuned performance while training 1.8 times faster. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Smendowski/PRISM.

</details>


### [263] [A Physics-Flavored Transformer Network for Parametrizing Contraction Dynamics of Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03927)
*Mattias Luber,Timo Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理风味神经网络（PFNN），融合拉伸指数物理模型与CNN-Transformer，通过合成数据预训练与无监督自对齐，实现工程化骨骼肌组织（ESMs）力-时间曲线的高保真动力学表型参数化，适用于多种收缩表型和细胞系（含杜兴氏肌营养不良模型），填补理想生物物理与噪声体外数据鸿沟，为高通量生物物理研究提供鲁棒工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ESMs功能表征依赖峰值力等简化指标，丢失关键动力学信息；机理模型数学复杂度高，阻碍大规模应用与推广；亟需自动化、可扩展的动力学表型分析工具，从力-时间曲线直接提取物理意义参数，弥合理想生物物理模型与噪声实验数据的差距。

Method: 构建物理风味神经网络（PFNN），将拉伸指数物理模型嵌入CNN-Transformer架构，实现从力-时间序列到物理参数的端到端映射。采用混合训练范式：先在合成数据上预训练建立“物理直觉”，再在无标签真实数据上进行无监督自对齐微调，利用物理约束实现自监督学习。

Result: PFNN在多种收缩表型和细胞系（含杜兴氏肌营养不良模型）上实现高保真参数化，参数估计精度显著优于纯数据驱动基线；混合训练有效缓解真实标注数据稀缺问题，模型泛化至未见实验条件；流程可扩展，支持高通量动力学表型分析。

Conclusion: PFNN通过物理先验与深度学习融合，实现ESMs动力学表型的自动化、高保真量化，解决了机理模型复杂度与数据驱动方法可解释性之间的矛盾，为高通量生物物理筛选和疾病建模提供了鲁棒、可推广的工具，推动了体外肌肉工程向定量精准医学转化。

Abstract: Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues (ESMs) have become a key structure for biomedical disease modeling and pharmacological screening, yet their functional characterization often relies on simplistic metrics like peak force, discarding critical kinetic information. This is partially due to the high level of mathematical complexity which mechanistic models introduce to capture these dynamics. Hence, exactly the complexity prevents scalable application and widespread adaptation in the field. Here we present a Physics-Flavored Neural Network (PFNN) that automates the kinetic phenotyping of ESMs. Our architecture integrates a stretched-exponential physical model into a CNN-Transformer, enabling the extraction of physically meaningful parameters directly from force-time profiles. To address the scarcity of labeled biological data, we employ a hybrid training paradigm: the model develops a "physical intuition" on synthetic data before undergoing unsupervised self-alignment on unlabeled real-world measurements. Our results demonstrate that this physics-flavored approach achieves high-fidelity parameterization across diverse contractile phenotypes and cell lines, including Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy models. Our scalable, self-improving pipeline bridges the gap between idealized biophysics and noisy \emph{in vitro} data, providing a robust tool for high-throughput biophysical research.

</details>


### [264] [Latent Reward Registers for Diffusion Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03929)
*Yuanshen Guan,Zipeng Feng,Zhiwei Xiong,Peiqin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Reward Registers（潜在奖励寄存器），通过在冻结的 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 输入序列前添加可学习的、位置无关的 register token，直接从中间噪声潜变量估计终端偏好，获得稠密可微的奖励信号。基于此，提出两种对齐策略：训练时的 Reward-Gradient On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD)，通过蒸馏奖励引导的在策略更新，避免昂贵的策略梯度采样；推理时的 Reward-Guided Sampling (RGS)，利用幅度匹配的奖励梯度引导采样轨迹，无需参数更新。实验显示：在高噪声水平 (u=0.8) 下，registers 在潜在奖励模型中达到最高配对准确率；RG-OPD 超越在线强化学习基线，GPU 小时数降低高达 33 倍；RGS 在免训练方法中建立新 SOTA，同时提升对齐度和感知指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与人类偏好对齐通常依赖最终生成样本上的稀疏终端奖励，导致在多步去噪过程中面临严重的时序信用分配难题，难以将奖励信号有效回传到中间步骤。

Method: 1. Latent Reward Registers：向冻结 DiT 的输入序列前拼接可学习、位置无关的 register token，作为独立读出机制，直接从中间噪声潜变量预测终端偏好，不改变生成器的隐藏状态或速度场，产出全去噪过程的稠密可微奖励。2. RG-OPD：利用该稠密奖励梯度，在在策略轨迹上蒸馏奖励引导的更新，绕过标准策略梯度所需的昂贵 rollout。3. RGS：推理阶段用幅度匹配的奖励梯度引导采样轨迹，无需更新模型参数。

Result: • 高噪声 (u=0.8) 下，registers 在评测的潜在奖励模型中达到最高配对准确率。• RG-OPD 性能优于在线强化学习基线，训练算力消耗降低最多 33 倍。• RGS 在免训练对齐方法中刷新 SOTA，同时严格提升对齐指标与感知质量指标。

Conclusion: Latent Reward Registers 通过轻量级 register token 实现了从中间潜变量到终端偏好的直接映射，提供了全过程的稠密奖励信号，配合 RG-OPD 与 RGS 两种互补策略，在训练高效性与免训练推理质量上均取得显著突破，为扩散模型偏好对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Aligning diffusion models with human preferences usually relies on a sparse terminal reward evaluated on the final generated samples, presenting a severe temporal credit-assignment challenge across the multi-step denoising process. We propose Latent Reward Registers, a mechanism that estimates terminal preference directly from intermediate noisy latents by prepending learnable, position-free register tokens to the input sequence of a frozen Diffusion Transformer (DiT). This independent readout mechanism extracts latent reward evidence without altering the generator's hidden states or velocity field. The resulting dense, differentiable reward signal throughout the full denoising process facilitates two alignment strategies. For training, Reward-Gradient On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD) distills reward-guided updates along on-policy trajectories, bypassing the computationally expensive rollouts of standard policy gradients. For inference, Reward-Guided Sampling (RGS) steers trajectories via magnitude-matched reward gradients without parameter updates. Empirically, at high noise levels (u = 0.8), the registers reach the highest pairwise accuracy among the evaluated latent reward models. Furthermore, RG-OPD outperforms online reinforcement learning baselines while reducing GPU hours by up to 33x, and RGS establishes a new state-of-the-art among training-free methods, strictly enhancing both alignment and perceptual metrics. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/Guanys-dar/latent-reward-register

</details>


### [265] [Muon Meets Mamba: Spectral Optimization for State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03941)
*Arslan Battalov,Karim Kramin,Alexander Markotenko,Sofia Sinitsina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Muon 优化器在 Mamba-2 状态空间模型上的表现，发现 Muon 仅在输出投影层使用时效果最佳，主要优势在于提高 token 效率，且该优势在不同语料、token 预算及过训练条件下均稳健存在，但不能用条件数改善来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器通过 Newton-Schulz 迭代正交化权重矩阵更新，在 Transformer 模型上表现优异，但其在状态空间模型（如 Mamba-2）上的行为鲜有报道，因此需要系统对比 Muon 与 AdamW 在 Mamba-2 上的表现。

Method: 在 Mamba-2 130M 模型上，采用受控实验协议，仅改变哪些权重组使用 Muon 而其他超参数保持一致，分别在输入投影、输出投影、两者同时使用 Muon 等设置下与 AdamW 基线对比，并在两个语料库、两个 token 预算及超出计算最优点的训练延长条件下评估。

Result: Muon 仅应用于输出投影层时性能提升最大，优于应用于输入投影或两者同时应用；优势主要体现为 token 效率提升，且跨语料、跨 token 预算、过训练阶段均稳健；Muon 会降低其作用层的条件数，但条件数改善较好的输入投影层并非受益层，故条件数不能解释性能增益。

Conclusion: 在 Mamba-2 中，Muon 的收益高度局部化：仅在输出投影层使用即可获得最佳 token 效率提升，且该现象普遍且鲁棒，但其机制与条件数优化无关，提示后续需探索更深层的优化动力学解释。

Abstract: Muon is a recent optimizer that orthogonalizes the update to each weight matrix with a Newton-Schulz iteration, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm. Almost all the evidence for it comes from Transformer models, and its behavior on state-space models is largely unreported. We compare Muon with AdamW on Mamba-2 130M under a controlled protocol that varies only which weight groups are trained with Muon. The benefit is localized. Muon on the output projection alone beats Muon on the input projection or on both. The advantage is mainly one of token efficiency. It holds on two corpora and two token budgets, and persists when training continues well past the compute-optimal point. Conditioning does not explain the gain. Muon lowers the condition number of whichever projection it trains, but the better-conditioned input projection is not the one that helps.

</details>


### [266] [Assessment of Conditional Diffusion Model for Synthetic Histopathology Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03990)
*Seyed Kahaki,Shijie Li,Weijie Chen,Nicholas Petrick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究合成组织病理图像的评估指标，指出基于ImageNet预训练的FID和IS指标在病理图像上存在局限，提出使用病理基础模型预训练的改进FID和IS，结合精确率-召回率指标，通过扩散模型生成不同质量的合成数据，并与下游核分割任务性能（AJI+、Dice）相关性分析，发现病理特定指标判别力更强，且生成数据的多样性比单张图像视觉保真度对分割性能影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成组织病理图像生成可缓解计算病理学中的数据稀缺问题，但现有评估指标（如FID、IS）依赖ImageNet预训练特征提取器，可能无法充分评估病理图像合成质量，需开发领域特定评估方法。

Method: 采用条件去噪扩散模型在四个基准数据集上训练，两阶段训练策略生成系统变化质量的合成数据集；提出基于病理基础模型预训练的改进FID和IS指标，补充精确率-召回率指标；测量合成数据质量指标与下游核分割性能（AJI+、Dice系数）的相关性。

Result: 改进的Inception Score与下游任务性能相关性显著提高（与AJI+相关系数r=0.6096，p=0.0122），远优于原始IS（r=0.0708，p=0.7944）；病理特定指标具有更好的判别力；增加生成训练数据的多样性比提升单张图像视觉保真度与分割模型性能正相关性更高。

Conclusion: 针对病理图像的领域特定评估指标能更有效反映合成数据对下游任务的价值，未来合成数据生成应更关注数据多样性而非单纯追求视觉逼真度。

Abstract: Synthetic histopathology image generation has emerged as an approach that may address data scarcity in computational pathology, yet current evaluation methodologies may not fully assess synthetic data quality for medical applications. This work investigates and addresses limitations in existing evaluation metrics, investigating an approach for assessing synthetic histopathology image quality through domain-specific metrics and downstream task validation. We show that conventional synthetic data evaluation metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) may have limitations when applied to histopathology images due to their reliance on ImageNet-pretrained feature extractors. To address these limitations, we propose for consideration modified FID and IS approaches utilizing foundation models pretrained on digital pathology datasets, supplemented by precision-recall based metrics as part of an additional quality assessment. Using conditional denoising diffusion models trained on four benchmark datasets, with a two-step training approach, we generated synthetic datasets with systematically varied quality characteristics. We also measured the correlation between the synthetic data quality metrics with downstream nuclei segmentation performance using common metrics including the aggregated Jaccard index (AJI+) and the Dice coefficient. The study results suggest that pathology-specific metrics may provide improved discriminative power. Specifically, the modified Inception Score indicates higher correlation with downstream task performance (r=0.6096 with AJI+, p=0.0122), compared to the original IS (r=0.0708, p=0.7944). Our observations indicate that increasing the variety of generated training data has a higher positive correlation with segmentation model performance than improving the visual fidelity of individual generated images.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Search, Inspect, Fetch: Exploiting Boolean Retrieval for Deep-Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02751)
*Shuai Wang,Haodong Chen,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIEVE是一种基于字段布尔检索(BQL)的搜索-检查-获取接口，通过利用网页的结构化字段（标题、标题、章节、元数据）过滤候选文档，仅获取选定章节，在三个QA数据集上实现比传统搜索-访问工作流更高的准确率，同时减少20.7%-50.6%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究代理采用搜索-访问工作流，检索并阅读整个页面，忽略了网页暴露的可寻址结构（标题、标题、章节、元数据），导致无法直接约束检索到文档字段，且常将无关页面内容引入上下文，降低效率和准确性。

Method: 提出SIEVE接口，由字段布尔检索(BQL)驱动：1) 在文档字段上过滤候选文档；2) 对通过的候选集进行排序；3) 展示结构丰富的结果卡片供代理检查；4) 仅获取代理选定的章节，而非整个页面。

Result: 在三个QA集合上，SIEVE的准确率均高于各集合上最准确的传统搜索-访问配置，同时使用20.7%-50.6%更少的token。进一步分析表明BQL过滤改进了所有测试的排序器，且准确率-上下文优势在不同检索器选择和代理主干中持续存在。

Conclusion: SIEVE通过利用网页结构化字段和字段布尔检索，显著提升了深度研究代理的检索效率和回答准确性，大幅减少token消耗，且具有良好的泛化性，适用于不同检索器和代理架构。

Abstract: Existing deep-research agents use a search-visit workflow that retrieves and reads whole pages, without considering the addressable structure that web sources expose through titles, headings, sections, and metadata. This prevents agents from directly constraining retrieval to document fields and often carries irrelevant page content into their context. We introduce SIEVE, a search-inspect-fetch interface driven by fielded Boolean retrieval (BQL). SIEVE filters candidates over document fields, ranks the admitted set, presents structure-rich result cards for inspection, and fetches only selected sections. Across three QA collections, SIEVE achieves higher accuracy than the most accurate conventional Search-Visit configuration on each collection while using 20.7-50.6% fewer tokens. Further analyses show that BQL filtering improves all tested rankers and that the accuracy-context advantage persists across retriever choices and agent backbones. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ielab/skim-search-agent.

</details>
